## 四、工具调用合理性结论

- **计划一致性：7/7 步骤全达成**，无遗漏、无杜撰、无跳步。
- **合理性**：全程命中专用工具（searchweb / fetchpage / write / edit），shell 仅用于 3 个正当场景（批量备份、执行脚本、存在性校验），**无绕路替代专用工具**。
- **冗余率 4.8%（1/21）**：唯一纯冗余是 write 统计脚本首次因缺逗号语法报错后重写；另有 2 项可优化项（listdir 大目录低价值、可用脚本打印省去 read JSON）。
- **3 次防御性调用均必要**：find 预检发现三个同名旧手册已存在（7,950/13,547/15,746 字节）→ 先备份至 `backup_manuals_20261004_141238\` 再覆盖，避免不可逆丢失。
- **质量把关 1 次**：edit 修正 Go 手册 `_ = <---chan` → `_ = <-ch` 语法错误。
- **并行调度有效**：B1（5 次）、B2（3 次抓取，最坏 3×40s 压缩为 1 次往返）、B5（3 次手册写入）共节省约 9 次串行往返；依赖顺序（备份→覆盖、写脚本→运行、统计→报告、写入→确认）全部正确。
- **改进建议**：脚本落盘前用 `ast.parse` 自检语法；大目录探查用 `find` 替代 `listdir`。

⚠️ 说明：手册中代码块为官方文档风格独立片段，未在本地编译运行验证（本机未确认 Go 工具链）；统计结果由脚本产出，重跑 `python E:\test_dir\_stats_three_manuals_20261004_1412.py` 可完整复现。

Go 编程语言 - Go 编程语言: 按 Enter 键激活/取消激活下拉菜单\n* 录制讲座\n先前活动的视频\n* 聚会 open_in_new\n结识其他当地的 Go 开发人员\n* 会议 open_in_new\n向来自世界各地的 Go 开发人员学习并与他们建立联系\n* Go 博客\nGo 项目的官方博客。\n* Go 项目\n从 Go 获得帮助并保持了解\n* 建立连接\n1. 学习\n安装最新版本的 Go\n安装最新版本的 Go。有关下载和安装 Go 编译器、工具和库的说明， 请查看安装文档。\n下载\n选定的教程 指导旅程 Qwiklabs 教程 培训 书籍\n选定的教程\n刚接触 Go 语言，不知道从何入手？\n* 文档\n  关于 Go 语言的所有知识。开始一个新项目或为现有的 Go 代码复习。\n  查看文档\n* Go 语言之旅\n  分四个部分对 Go 语言进行交互式介绍。每个部分都以一些练习结束，以便你可以练习所学内容。\n  开始之旅\n* Go 语言示例\n  Go 语言示例 是使用带注释的示例程序对 Go 语言进行实践性介绍。在处理任何 Go 语言项目时，这是一个很好的起点。\n  查看示例\n指导性学习之旅\n掌握了基础知识，想要了解更多内容？...(截断) [Parallel]\n    URL: https://go-lang.org.cn/learn\n  Go 学习路线| 26 年最新零基础到精通一条龙（万人收藏⭐️）: Go 学习路线| 26 年最新零基础到精通一条龙（万人收藏⭐️）\n2024年5月11日 — 学习资源 ; 2024 新版Go 入门教程：十年大厂程序员讲解，通俗易懂 ; Go 语言中文网：Go 语言中文学习资源 ; 李文周的博客：免费的Go 语言学习笔记和教程 [Parallel]\n    URL: https://www.codefather.cn/course/1789189862986850306/section/1789190243125010434\n✓ 无截断-完整", 'other_data': {'retry_count': 0}}]`

执行结果: 成功\n观察: 获取网页内容成功 - 获取网页https://docs.python.org/zh-cn/3/tutorial/index.html内容成功: markdown格式，HTTP 200\n详情:\n── 网页正文 ── https://docs.python.org/zh-cn/3/tutorial/index.html (markdown)\n# Python 教程¶\n\n小技巧\n\n本教程被设计为针对新入门 Python 语言的 *程序员*，而 **不是** 新入门编程的 *初学者*。\n\nPython 是一门易于学习、功能强大的编程语言。它提供了高效的高级数据结构，还能简单有效地面向对象编程。Python 优雅的语法和动态类型以及解释型语言的本质，使它成为多数平台上写脚本和快速开发应用的理想语言。\n\nPython 解释器及丰富的标准库针对所有主流系统平台以源代码或二进制形式在 Python 网站 https://www.python.org/ 上免费提供，并可自由分发。 该网站还包含许多免费的第三方 Python 模块、程序和工具以及附加文档的分享链接。\n\nPython 解释器易于扩展，使用 C 或 C++（或其他 C 能调用的语言）即可为 Python 扩展新功能和数据类型。Python 也可用作定制软件中的扩展程序语言。\n\n本教程非正式地介绍了 Python 语言和系统的基本概念和特性。 请注意它预期你对于编程的总体概念有基本的了解。 准备好一个 Python 解释器随时上手练习会很有帮助，但所有的示例都是完备自足的，因此本教程也可以离线阅读。\n\n有关标准对象与模块的说明，请参阅 Python built-ins reference 和 The Python standard library。 Python 语言参考手册 给出了更正规的语言定义。 要用 C 或 C++ 编写扩展，请阅读 扩展和嵌入 Python 解释器 和 Python/C API 参考手册。 此外还有一些深入讲解 Python 的书籍。\n\n本教程对每一个功能的介绍并不完整，甚至没有涉及全部常用功能，只是介绍了 Python 中最值得学习的功能，旨在让读者快速感受一下 Python 的特色。学完本教程的读者可以阅读和编写 Python 模块和程序，也可以继续学习 The Python standard library。\n\n强烈推荐阅读 术语对照表。\n\n- 1. 课前甜点\n- 2. 使用 Python 的解释器\n- 3. This page will give you an introduction to 80% of the React concepts that you will use on a daily basis.\n\n### You will learn\n\n- How to create and nest components\n- How to add markup and styles\n- How to display data\n- How to render conditions and lists\n- How to respond to events and update the screen\n- How to share data between components\n\n## Creating and nesting components\n\nReact apps are made out of *components*. A component is a piece of the UI (user interface) that has its own logic and appearance. A component can be as small as a button, or as large as an entire page.\n\nReact components are JavaScript functions that return markup:\n\n```\nfunction MyButton() {\n return (\n <button>I\'m a button</button>\n );\n}\n```\nNow that you’ve declared `MyButton`, you can nest it into another component:\n\n```\nexport default function MyApp() {\n return (\n <div>\n <h1>Welcome to my app</h1>\n <MyButton />\n </div>\n );\n}\n```\nNotice that `<MyButton />` starts with a capital letter. That’s how you know it’s a React component. React component names must always start with a capital letter, while HTML tags must be lowercase.\n\nHave a look at the result:\n\nfunction MyButton() { return ( <button> I\'m a button </button> ); } export default function MyApp() { return ( <div> <h1>Welcome to my app</h1> <MyButton /> </div> ); }\n\nThe `export default` keywords specify the main component in the file. If you’re not familiar with some piece of JavaScript syntax, MDN and javascript.info have great references.\n\n## Writing markup with JSX\n\nThe markup syntax you’ve seen above is called *JSX*. It is optional, but most React projects use JSX for its convenience. All of the tools we recommend for local development support JSX out of the box.\n\nJSX is stricter than HTML. You have to close tags like `<br />`. Your component also can’t return multiple JSX tags. You have to wrap them into a shared parent, like a `<div>...</div>` or an empty `<>...</>` wrapper:\n\n```\nfunction AboutPage() {\n return (\n <>\n <h1>About</h1>\n <p>Hello there.<br />How do you do?</p>\n </>\n );\n}\n```\nIf you have a lot of HTML to port to JSX, you can use an online converter.\n\n## Adding styles\n\nIn React, you specify a CSS class with `className`. It works the same way as the HTML `class` attribute:\n\n`<img className="avatar" />`Then you write the CSS rules for it in a separate CSS file:\n\n```\n/* In your CSS */\n.avatar {\n border-radius: 50%;\n}\n```\nReact does not prescribe how you add CSS files. In the simplest case, you’ll add a `<link>` tag to your HTML. If you use a build tool or a framework, consult its documentation to learn how to add a CSS file to your project.\n\n## Displaying data\n\nJSX lets you put markup into JavaScript. Curly braces let you “escape back” into JavaScript so that you can embed some variable from your code and display it to the user. For example, this will display `user.name`:\n\n```\nreturn (\n <h1>\n {user.name}\n </h1>\n);\n```\nYou can also “escape into JavaScript” from JSX attributes, but you have to use curly braces *instead of* quotes. For example, `className="avatar"` passes the `"avatar"` string as the CSS class, but `src={user.imageUrl}` reads the JavaScript `user.imageUrl` variable value, and then passes that value as the `src` attribute:\n\n```\nreturn (\n <img\n className="avatar"\n src={user.imageUrl}\n />\n);\n```\nYou can put more complex expressions inside the JSX curly braces too, for example, string concatenation:\n\nconst user = { name: \'Hedy Lamarr\', imageUrl: \'https://react.dev/images/docs/scientists/yXOvdOSs.jpg\', imageSize: 90, }; export default function Profile() { return ( <> <h1>{user.name}</h1> <img className="avatar" src={user.imageUrl} alt={\'Photo of \' + user.name} style={{ width: user.imageSize, height: user.imageSize }} /> </> ); }\n\nIn the above example, `style={{}}` is not a special syntax, but a regular `{}` object inside the `style={ }` JSX curly braces. You can use the `style` attribute when your styles depend on JavaScript variables.\n\n## Conditional rendering\n\nIn React, there is no special syntax for writing conditions. Instead, you’ll use the same techniques as you use when writing regular JavaScript code. For example, you can use an `if` statement to conditionally include JSX:\n\n```\nlet content;\nif (isLoggedIn) {\n content = <AdminPanel />;\n} else {\n content = <LoginForm />;\n}\nreturn (\n <div>\n {content}\n </div>\n);\n```\nIf you prefer more compact code, you can use the conditional `?` operator. Unlike `if`, it works inside JSX:\n\n```\n<div>\n {isLoggedIn ? (\n <AdminPanel />\n ) : (\n <LoginForm />\n )}\n</div>\n```\nWhen you don’t need the `else` branch, you can also use a shorter logical `&&` syntax:\n\n```\n<div>\n {isLoggedIn && <AdminPanel />}\n</div>\n```\nAll of these approaches also work for conditionally specifying attributes. If you’re unfamiliar with some of this JavaScript syntax, you can start by always using `if...else`.\n\n## Rendering lists\n\nYou will rely on JavaScript features like `for` loop and the array `map()` function to render lists of components.\n\nFor example, let’s say you have an array of products:\n\n```\nconst products = [\n { title: \'Cabbage\', id: 1 },\n { title: \'Garlic\', id: 2 },\n { title: \'Apple\', id: 3 },\n];\n```\nInside your component, use the `map()` function to transform an array of products into an array of `<li>` items:\n\n```\nconst listItems = products.map(product =>\n <li key={product.id}>\n {product.title}\n </li>\n);\nreturn (\n <ul>{listItems}</ul>\n);\n```\nNotice how `<li>` has a `key` attribute. For each item in a list, you should pass a string or a number that uniquely identifies that item among its siblings. Usually, a key should be coming from your data, such as a database ID. React uses your keys to know what happened if you later insert, delete, or reorder the items.\n\nconst products = [ { title: \'Cabbage\', isFruit: false, id: 1 }, { title: \'Garlic\', isFruit: false, id: 2 }, { title: \'Apple\', isFruit: true, id: 3 }, ]; export default function ShoppingList() { const listItems = products.map(product => <li key={product.id} style={{ color: product.isFruit ? \'magenta\' : \'darkgreen\' }} > {product.title} </li> ); return ( <ul>{listItems}</ul> ); }\n\n## Responding to events\n\nYou can respond to events by declaring *event handler* functions inside your components:\n\n```\nfunction MyButton() {\n function handleClick() {\n alert(\'You clicked me!\');\n }\n return (\n <button onClick={handleClick}>\n Click me\n </button>\n );\n}\n```\nNotice how `onClick={handleClick}` has no parentheses at the end! Do not *call* the event handler function: you only need to *pass it down*. React will call your event handler when the user clicks the button.\n\n## Updating the screen\n\nOften, you’ll want your component to “remember” some information and display it. For example, maybe you want to count the number of times a button is clicked. To do this, add *state* to your component.\n\nFirst, import `useState` from React:\n\n`import { useState } from \'react\';`Now you can declare a *state variable* inside your component:\n\n```\nfunction MyButton() {\n const [count, setCount] = useState(0);\n  ... 还有 104 行（仅展示前 200 行）\n⚠ 已截断', 'other_data': {'retry_count': 0}}, {'tool_name': 'fetchpage', 'llm_data': {'summary': '获取网页https://go.dev/doc/effective_go内容成功: markdown格式，HTTP 200', 'action': {'tool': 'fetchpage', 'tool_zh': '获取网页', 'target': 'https://go.dev/doc/effective_go', 'params': {'url': 'https://go.dev/doc/effective_go', 'extract_format': 'markdown', 'js_render': False, 'timeout': 40, 'prompt': 'Effective Go 核心概念：变量、常量、控制语句、结构体、接口、方法、错误处理、并发 goroutine、channel、包与导入'}}, 'status': {'exec_code': 'success', 'message': '获取网页内容成功', 'code': '', 'detail': '', 'hint': ''}, 'duration_ms': 1499, 'metrics': {'status_code': {'value': 200, 'text': 'HTTP 200'}}}, 'llm_data_text': '{\n  "summary": "获取网页https://go.dev/doc/effective_go内容成功: markdown格式，HTTP 200",\n  "action": {\n    "tool": "fetchpage",\n    "tool_zh": "获取网页",\n    "target": "https://go.dev/doc/effective_go",\n    "params": {\n      "url": "https://go.dev/doc/effective_go",\n      "extract_format": "markdown",\n      "js_render": false,\n      "timeout": 40,\n      "prompt": "Effective Go 核心概念：变量、常量、控制语句、结构体、接口、方法、错误处理、并发 goroutine、channel、包与导入"\n    }\n  },\n  "status": {\n    "exec_code": "success",\n    "message": "获取网页内容成功",\n    "code": "",\n    "detail": "",\n    "hint": ""\n  },\n  "duration_ms": 1499,\n  "metrics": {\n    "status_code": {\n      "value": 200,\n      "text": "HTTP 200"\n    }\n  }\n}', 'data_text': '工具执行: 获取网页 调用工具-fetchpage,处理对象-https://go.dev/doc/effective_go - 执行结果: 成功\n观察: 获取网页内容成功 - 获取网页https://go.dev/doc/effective_go内容成功: markdown格式，HTTP 200\n详情:\n── 网页正文 ── https://go.dev/doc/effective_go (markdown)\n# Effective Go\n\n**Note:**This document was written for Go\'s release in 2009 and is not actively updated. While it remains a good guide for using the core language, it does not cover significant changes to the language (generics), ecosystem (modules), or libraries added since. See issue 28782 for context. For a complete list of changes, see the release notes.\n\n## Introduction\n\nGo is an open-source programming language that focuses on simplicity, reliability, and efficiency, specifically designed to make it easy to build software at scale. Although it borrows ideas from existing languages, it has unusual properties that make effective Go programs different in character from programs written in its relatives. A straightforward translation of a C++ or Java program into Go is unlikely to produce a satisfactory result—Java programs are written in Java, not Go. On the other...(截断)\n\nThis document gives tips for writing clear, idiomatic Go code. It augments the language specification, the Tour of Go, and How to Write Go Code, all of which you should read first.\n\n### Examples\n\nThe Go package sources are intended to serve not only as the core library but also as examples of how to use the language. Moreover, many of the packages contain working, self-contained executable examples you can run directly from the go.dev web site, such as this one (if necessary, click on the word "Example" to open it up). If you have a question about how to approach a problem or how something might be implemented, the documentation, code and examples in the library can provide answers, idea...(截断)\n\n## Formatting\n\nFormatting issues are the most contentious but the least consequential. People can adapt to different formatting styles but it\'s better if they don\'t have to, and less time is devoted to the topic if everyone adheres to the same style. The problem is how to approach this Utopia without a long prescriptive style guide.\n\nWith Go we take an unusual\napproach and let the machine\ntake care of most formatting issues.\nThe `gofmt` program\n(also available as `go fmt`, which\noperates at the package level rather than source file level)\nreads a Go program\nand emits the source in a standard style of indentation\nand vertical alignment, retaining and if necessary\nreformatting comments.\nIf you want to know how to handle some new layout\nsituation, run `gofmt`; if the answer doesn\'t\nseem right, rearrange your program (or file a bug about `gofmt`),\ndon\'t work around it.\n\nAs an example, there\'s no need to spend time lining up\nthe comments on the fields of a structure.\n`Gofmt` will do that for you. Given the\ndeclaration\n\n```\ntype T struct {\n name string // name of the object\n value int // its value\n}\n```\n`gofmt` will line up the columns:\n\n```\ntype T struct {\n name string // name of the object\n value int // its value\n}\n```\nAll Go code in the standard packages has been formatted with `gofmt`.\n\nSome formatting details remain. Very briefly:\n\n- Indentation\n- We use tabs for indentation and `gofmt`emits them by default. Use spaces only if you must.\n- Line length\n- Go has no line length limit. Don\'t worry about overflowing a punched card. If a line feels too long, wrap it and indent with an extra tab.\n- Parentheses\n- \n Go needs fewer parentheses than C and Java: control structures (`if`,`for`,`switch`) do not have parentheses in their syntax. Also, the operator precedence hierarchy is shorter and clearer, sox<<8 + y<<16 means what the spacing implies, unlike in the other languages.\n\n## Commentary\n\nGo provides C-style `/* */` block comments\nand C++-style `//` line comments.\nLine comments are the norm;\nblock comments appear mostly as package comments, but\nare useful within an expression or to disable large swaths of code.\n\nComments that appear before top-level declarations, with no intervening newlines, are considered to document the declaration itself. These “doc comments” are the primary documentation for a given Go package or command. For more about doc comments, see “Go Doc Comments”.\n\n## Names\n\nNames are as important in Go as in any other language. They even have semantic effect: the visibility of a name outside a package is determined by whether its first character is upper case. It\'s therefore worth spending a little time talking about naming conventions in Go programs.\n\n### Package names\n\nWhen a package is imported, the package name becomes an accessor for the contents. After\n\nimport "bytes"\n\nthe importing package can talk about `bytes.Buffer`. It\'s\nhelpful if everyone using the package can use the same name to refer to\nits contents, which implies that the package name should be good:\nshort, concise, evocative. By convention, packages are given\nlower case, single-word names; there should be no need for underscores\nor mixedCaps.\nErr on the side of brevity, since everyone using your\npackage will be typing that name.\nAnd don\'t worry about collisions *a priori*.\nThe package name is only the default name for imports; it need not be unique\nacross all source code, and in the rare case of a collision the\nimporting package can choose a different name to use locally.\nIn any case, confusion is rare because the file name in the import\ndetermines just which package is being used.\n\nAnother convention is that the package name is the base name of\nits source directory;\nthe package in `src/encoding/base64`\nis imported as `"encoding/base64"` but has name `base64`,\nnot `encoding_base64` and not `encodingBase64`.\n\nThe importer of a package will use the name to refer to its contents,\nso exported names in the package can use that fact\nto avoid repetition.\n(Don\'t use the `import .` notation, which can simplify\ntests that must run outside the package they are testing, but should otherwise be avoided.)\nFor instance, the buffered reader type in the `bufio` package is called `Reader`,\nnot `BufReader`, because users see it as `bufio.Reader`,\nwhich is a clear, concise name.\nMoreover,\nbecause imported entities are always addressed with their package name, `bufio.Reader`\ndoes not conflict with `io.Reader`.\nSimilarly, the function to make new instances of `ring.Ring`—which\nis the definition of a *constructor* in Go—would\nnormally be called `NewRing`, but since\n`Ring` is the only type exported by the package, and since the\npackage is called `ring`, it\'s called just `New`,\nwhich clients of the package see as `ring.New`.\nUse the package structure to help you choose good names.\n\nAnother short example is `once.Do`;\n`once.Do(setup)` reads well and would not be improved by\nwriting `once.DoOrWaitUntilDone(setup)`.\nLong names don\'t automatically make things more readable.\nA helpful doc comment can often be more valuable than an extra long name.\n\n### Getters\n\nGo doesn\'t provide automatic support for getters and setters.\nThere\'s nothing wrong with providing getters and setters yourself,\nand it\'s often appropriate to do so, but it\'s neither idiomatic nor necessary\nto put `Get` into the getter\'s name. If you have a field called\n`owner` (lower case, unexported), the getter method should be\ncalled `Owner` (upper case, exported), not `GetOwner`.\nThe use of upper-case names for export provides the hook to discriminate\nthe field from the method.\nA setter function, if needed, will likely be called `SetOwner`.\nBoth names read well in practice:\n\n```\nowner := obj.Owner()\nif owner != user {\n obj.SetOwner(user)\n}\n```\n### Interface names\n\nBy convention, one-method interfaces are named by\nthe method name plus an -er suffix or similar modification\nto construct an agent noun: `Reader`,\n`Writer`, `Formatter`,\n`CloseNotifier` etc.\n\nThere are a number of such names and it\'s productive to honor them and the function\nnames they capture.\n`Read`, `Write`, `Close`, `Flush`,\n`String` and so on have\ncanonical signatures and meanings. To avoid confusion,\ndon\'t give your method one of those names unless it\nhas the same signature and meaning.\nConversely, if your type implements a method with the\nsame meaning as a method on a well-known type,\ngive it the same name and signature;\ncall your string-converter method `String` not `ToString`.\n\n### MixedCaps\n\nFinally, the convention in Go is to use `MixedCaps`\nor `mixedCaps` rather than underscores to write\nmultiword names.\n\n## Semicolons\n\nLike C, Go\'s formal grammar uses semicolons to terminate statements, but unlike in C, those semicolons do not appear in the source. Instead the lexer uses a simple rule to insert semicolons automatically as it scans, so the input text is mostly free of them.\n\nThe rule is this. If the last token before a newline is an identifier\n(which includes words like `int` and `float64`),\na basic literal such as a number or string constant, or one of the\ntokens\n\nbreak continue fallthrough return ++ -- ) }\n\nthe lexer always inserts a semicolon after the token. This could be summarized as, “if the newline comes after a token that could end a statement, insert a semicolon”.\n\nA semicolon can also be omitted immediately before a closing brace, so a statement such as\n\n```\n go func() { for { dst <- <-src } }()\n```\nneeds no semicolons.\nIdiomatic Go programs have semicolons only in places such as\n`for` loop clauses, to separate the initializer, condition, and\ncontinuation elements. They are also necessary to separate multiple\nstatements on a line, should you write code that way.\n\nOne consequence of the semicolon insertion rules\n  ... 还有 2546 行（仅展示前 200 行）\n⚠ 已截断', 'other_data': {'retry_count': 0}}]`

- 参数: `{"path": "E:\\test_dir\\python_manual.md", "content": "# Python 学习手册\n\n> 手册版本：v2.0 ｜ 生成时间：2026-10-04 14:12 ｜ 整理：SenseNova\n> 核心来源：Python 3.14 官方教程 <https://docs.python.org/zh-cn/3/tutorial/>、python.org/doc、PEP 8、PyPI 打包指南\n> 官方定位：教程面向\"新入门 Python 语言的**程序员**\"，而非零基础编程初学者。\n\n## 1. 语言概述与定位\n\n- 解释型、动态类型、面向对象；语法优雅、缩进即结构。\n- \"Batteries included\"：标准库极其丰富（`os`/`sys`/`re`/`json`/`datetime`/`pathlib`/`concurrent.futures` 等）。\n- 适用场景：脚本自动化、Web 后端（Django/Flask/FastAPI）、数据科学（NumPy/Pandas）、AI/ML（PyTorch/TensorFlow）。\n- 版本现状：**3.14** 为最新稳定线，相对 3.13 性能提升 10%~60%，错误提示更友好。\n\n## 2. 环境准备\n\n```bash\npython --version          # 查看版本\npython -m venv .venv      # 创建虚拟环境\n.venv\\Scripts\\activate    # Windows 激活\npip install -r requirements.txt\npip freeze > requirements.txt\n```\n\n## 3. 基本语法\n\n### 3.1 动态类型与变量\n\n```python\nname = \"SenseNova\"   # str\nage = 6              # int\nscore = 98.5         # float\nok = True            # bool\npi = 3.14e0          # 指数记法\na = b = 10           # 多重绑定\nx, y, z = 1, 2, 3    # 解包\n```\n\n### 3.2 类型转换与常用内建\n\n```python\nint(\"42\")           # 字符串转整数\nstr(3.14)           # 数字转字符串\ntype(1)             # <class 'int'>\nlen([1, 2, 3])      # 3\nmax(1, 2, 3)        # 3\nsorted([3, 1, 2])   # [1, 2, 3]\nlist(range(5))      # [0, 1, 2, 3, 4]\n```\n\n### 3.3 输入输出与 f-string\n\n```python\nname = input(\"What is your name?\\n\")\nprint(f\"Hi, {name}.\")\nprint(\"a\", \"b\", sep=\"|\")\nprint(f\"{3.14159:.2f}\")    # 3.14\nprint(f\"{1000000:,}\")      # 1,000,000\nprint(f\"{0xFF:x}\")         # ff\n```\n\n## 4. 控制流\n\n```python\nif score >= 90:\n    grade = \"A\"\nelif score >= 60:\n    grade = \"B\"\nelse:\n    grade = \"C\"\n\nfor i, v in enumerate([\"a\", \"b\", \"c\"]):\n    print(i, v)\n\nn = 0\nwhile n < 5:\n    n += 1\n    if n == 3:\n        continue\n    print(n)\n```\n\n### 4.1 推导式（Python 特色）\n\n```python\nsquares = [x ** 2 for x in range(10)]\nevens = [x for x in range(20) if x % 2 == 0]\nd = {x: x * x for x in range(5)}\npairs = [(a, b) for a in (1, 2) for b in (3, 4)]\n```\n\n### 4.2 三元表达式与 match 语句\n\n```python\nabs_val = x if x > 0 else -x\n\nmatch cmd:\n    case \"quit\":\n        raise SystemExit(0)\n    case \"help\":\n        print(__doc__)\n    case _ if cmd.startswith(\"?\"):\n        print(\"unknown\")\n```\n\n## 5. 数据结构\n\n### 5.1 列表与元组\n\n```python\nlst = [1, 2, 3]\nlst.append(4)\nlst.sort(reverse=True)\nlst[1:3]          # 切片\nt = (1, 2, 3)     # 元组不可变\n# 推导式修改：lst = [x * 2 for x in lst]\n```\n\n### 5.2 字典与集合\n\n```python\nd = {\"name\": \"Py\", \"age\": 30}\nd[\"age\"] = 31\nd.get(\"city\", \"Beijing\")\nfor k, v in d.items():\n    print(k, v)\n\ns1, s2 = {1, 2, 3}, {2, 3, 4}\ns1 | s2           # 并集 {1, 2, 3, 4}\ns1 & s2           # 交集 {2, 3}\ns1 - s2           # 差集 {1}\n```\n\n### 5.3 字符串与 pathlib\n\n```python\ns = \" hello world \"\ns.strip()\ns.replace(\"hello\", \"hi\")\n\"{}\".format(1), f\"{1!s}\"\n\"abc\".split()\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\np = Path(\"data\") / \"out.txt\"\np.write_text(\"hi\")\np.read_text()\n```\n\n## 6. 函数\n\n```python\ndef greet(name, greeting=\"Hello\"):\n    \"\"\"Return a greeting string.\"\"\"\n    return f\"{greeting}, {name}!\"\n\ngreet(\"Py\", greeting=\"Hi\")\n```\n\n```python\ndef f(*args, kw=1, **kwargs):\n    print(args, kw, kwargs)\n\nf(1, 2, kw=3, extra=9)\n```\n\n```python\nadd = lambda a, b: a + b\n\nnums = [1, 2, 3, 4]\nlist(map(lambda x: x * 2, nums))\nlist(filter(lambda x: x > 2, nums))\nsum(map(str, nums))\n```\n\n### 6.1 装饰器\n\n```python\ndef log(func):\n    def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):\n        print(\"call\", func.__name__)\n        return func(*args, **kwargs)\n    return wrapper\n\n@log\ndef add(a, b):\n    return a + b\n```\n\n### 6.2 生成器与迭代器\n\n```python\ndef count(n):\n    i = 0\n    while i < n:\n        yield i\n        i += 1\n\nlist(count(5))\ngen = (x * x for x in range(3))   # 生成器表达式\n```\n\n## 7. 面向对象\n\n```python\nclass Dog:\n    species = \"Canis\"             # 类属性\n\n    def __init__(self, name, age):\n        self.name = name          # 实例属性\n        self.age = age\n\n    def bark(self):\n        return f\"{self.name} says woof\"\n\n    def __repr__(self):\n        return f\"Dog({self.name}, { self.age})\"\n\nd = Dog(\"Lucky\", 3)\nd.bark()\n```\n\n```python\nclass Animal:\n    def speak(self):\n        return \"...\"\n\nclass Cat(Animal):\n    def speak(self):\n        return \"meow\"\n\nfor a in (Dog(\"Lucky\", 3), Cat()):\n    print(type(a).__name__, a.speak())   # 多态\n```\n\n```python\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\n@dataclass\nclass Point:\n    x: float\n    y: float\n    def dist(self):\n        return (self.x ** 2 + self.y ** 2) ** 0.5\n\np = Point(3, 4)\np.dist()\n```\n\n### 7.1 上下文管理器\n\n```python\nclass File:\n    def __enter__(self):\n        return self\n\n    def __exit__(self, *exc):\n        print(\"closed\")\n\nwith File() as f:\n    pass\n```\n\n## 8. 异常处理\n\n```python\ntry:\n    v = 1 / 0\nexcept ZeroDivisionError as e:\n    print(\"ZeroDivisionError:\", e)\nexcept Exception as e:\n    print(\"other:\", e)\nelse:\n    print(\"no error\")\nfinally:\n    print(\"always\")\n```\n\n```python\nclass BizError(Exception):\n    def __init__(self, code, msg):\n        super().__init__(msg)\n        self.code = code\n\ntry:\n    raise BizError(404, \"not found\")\nexcept BizError as e:\n    print(e.code, e)\n```\n\n## 9. 文件、模块与标准库\n\n```python\nwith open(\"a.txt\", \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n    f.write(\"hello\\n\")\n\nwith open(\"a.txt\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n    for line in f:\n        print(line.strip())\n```\n\n```python\nimport json, csv, os, re, sys, datetime\nfrom collections import defaultdict, Counter\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nd = {\"a\": 1}\njson.dumps(d, ensure_ascii=False)\njson.loads('{\"a\": 1}')\n\nCounter(\"aab\").most_common(1)\nre.findall(r\"\\d+\", \"a1b2\")\ndatetime.datetime.now().isoformat()\n```\n\n```python\n# 导入：from module import func / import module as m\nimport urllib.request\nurllib.request.urlopen(\"https://python.org\").status\n```\n\n## 10. 类型注解\n\n```python\nfrom typing import List, Optional\n\ndef greet(names: List[str], times: int = 1) -> None:\n    for n in names:\n        print(n, times)\n\ndef find(xs: list[int], key: str) -> Optional[str]:\n    return next((x for x in xs if key in x), None)\n```\n\n## 11. 日志与并发\n\n```python\nimport logging\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\")\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\nlog.info(\"started\")\n```\n\n```python\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\nimport urllib.request\n\ndef fetch(url):\n    return urllib.request.urlopen(url).status\n\nwith ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as ex:\n    results = list(ex.map(fetch, [\"https://python.org\"] * 3))\n```\n\n## 12. 最佳实践（PEP 8 要点）\n\n| 规则 | 建议 |\n|---|---|\n| 缩进 | 4 个空格，不用 Tab |\n| 行宽 | 建议 ≤ 79 字符 |\n| 命名 | 变量/函数 `snake_case`，类 `CamelCase`，常量 `UPPER_CASE` |\n| 导入 | 一行一个模块，`import os` 而非 `from os import *` |\n| 空格 | 运算符两侧、逗号后加空格 |\n| 文档 | 模块/函数写 docstring |\n| 工具 | 用 `ruff` / `black` / `mypy` 统一格式与类型检查 |\n\n## 13. 推荐学习路径与资源\n\n1. **官方教程**：docs.python.org/zh-cn/3/tutorial/（循序渐进，示例完备自足）\n2. **语言参考**：docs.python.org/zh-cn/3/reference/（正规语法定义）\n3. **标准库参考**：docs.python.org/zh-cn/3/library/（\"batteries included\"）\n4. **PEP 8 / PEP 20（Beautiful is better than ugly）**：python.org/dev/peps/\n5. **PyPI 打包用户指南**：packaging.python.org\n6. **中文镜像**：pythonlang.cn/doc、pydocs.cn\n7. 进阶：《Fluent Python》《Effective Python》\n"}`
**步骤5: write**
- 参数: `{"path": "E:\\test_dir\\react_manual.md", "content": "# React 学习手册\n\n> 手册版本：v2.0 ｜ 生成时间：2026-10-04 14:12 ｜ 整理：SenseNova\n> 核心来源：React 官方文档 <https://react.dev/learn>（Quick Start 覆盖 80% 日常概念）、react.dev/reference/react/hooks、React 规则（Rules of React）\n> 当前主版本：React 19（19.1 系列）\n\n## 1. 心智模型\n\n- React 应用由**组件（Component）**组成：组件是拥有自身逻辑与外观的 UI 片段，可小到按钮、大到整页。\n- 组件就是**返回 markup 的 JavaScript 函数**。\n- React 是**声明式**的：你描述\"要渲染什么\"，React 决定\"如何显示\"。\n- 组件与 Hooks 只能由 React 调用（在 JSX 中），**不要像普通函数那样直接调用组件**。\n\n## 2. 组件与 JSX\n\n```jsx\nfunction MyButton() {\n  return (\n    <button>I'm a button</button>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n```jsx\nexport default function MyApp() {\n  return (\n    <div>\n      <h1>Welcome to my app</h1>\n      <MyButton />\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n> 约定：组件名必须**大写开头**（`<MyButton />`），HTML 标签小写。`export default` 指定文件主组件。\n\n### 2.1 JSX 语法约束\n\nJSX 比 HTML 更严格：自闭合标签要写 `<br />`；返回的 JSX 必须有单一父节点（`<div>` 或空片段 `<>...</>`）。\n\n```jsx\nfunction AboutPage() {\n  return (\n    <>\n      <h1>About</h1>\n      <p>Hello there.<br />How do you do?</p>\n    </>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n### 2.2 样式\n\nReact 中 CSS 类名用 `className`，规则写在单独的 CSS 文件里；依赖 JS 变量时用 `style`。\n\n```css\n/* In your CSS */\n.avatar {\n  border-radius: 50%;\n}\n```\n\n```jsx\nconst user = {\n  name: 'Hedy Lamarr',\n  imageUrl: 'https://react.dev/images/docs/scientists/yXOvdOSs.jpg',\n  imageSize: 90,\n};\n\nexport default function Profile() {\n  return (\n    <>\n      <h1>{user.name}</h1>\n      <img\n        className=\"avatar\"\n        src={user.imageUrl}\n        alt={'Photo of ' + user.name}\n        style={{ width: user.imageSize, height: user.imageSize }}\n      />\n    </>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n> JSX 中用 `{}` 插值 JS 表达式；属性里用 `{}` 而**不是**引号：`className=\"avatar\"` 传字符串，`src={user.imageUrl}` 读变量。\n\n## 3. Props 与数据传递\n\n```jsx\nfunction Greeting({ name, role }) {\n  return <p>{role} {name}</p>;\n}\n\nexport default function App() {\n  return <Greeting name=\"Ada\" role=\"Engineer\" />;\n}\n```\n\n```jsx\nfunction Card({ children }) {\n  return <section className=\"card\">{children}</section>;\n}\n\n<Card>\n  <h3>Profile</h3>\n  <p>Children prop 承载任意子节点</p>\n</Card>\n```\n\n## 4. State 与 useState\n\nReact 没有状态就无法\"记住\"信息。用 `useState` 为函数组件添加本地状态，React 会在多次渲染之间**保留该状态**。\n\n```jsx\nimport { useState } from 'react';\n\nfunction MyButton() {\n  const [count, setCount] = useState(0);\n  return (\n    <button onClick={() => setCount(count + 1)}>\n      You clicked {count} times\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n要点：\n- `useState` 返回一对值：当前状态 + 更新函数。\n- 更新状态后 **React 重渲染**，所以更新逻辑必须放在事件处理函数里，不能放在渲染体中直接改。\n- 传入与当前值相同的值时，React 用 `Object.is` 比较后跳过子渲染。\n\n```jsx\nimport { useState } from 'react';\n\nexport default function Form() {\n  const [name, setName] = useState('');\n  return (\n    <form>\n      <label>\n        Name:{' '}\n        <input value={name} onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)} />\n      </label>\n      <p>Hello, {name || 'stranger'}!</p>\n    </form>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n> 受控组件：`value` + `onChange` 让输入框完全由 state 驱动。\n\n## 5. 事件处理\n\n```jsx\nfunction MyButton() {\n  function handleClick() {\n    alert('You clicked me!');\n  }\n  return (\n    <button onClick={handleClick}>Click me</button>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n> `onClick={handleClick}` **末尾没有括号**：只传递函数，不要调用它；React 会在点击时调用。\n\n## 6. 条件渲染\n\nReact 没有专门的条件语法，直接用 JavaScript：`if...else`、三元 `? :`、逻辑与 `&&`。\n\n```jsx\nlet content;\nif (isLoggedIn) {\n  content = <AdminPanel />;\n} else {\n  content = <LoginForm />;\n}\nreturn <div>{content}</div>;\n```\n\n```jsx\n<div>\n  {isLoggedIn ? <AdminPanel /> : <LoginForm />}\n  {isFavorite && <FavoriteIcon />}\n</div>\n```\n\n## 7. 列表渲染与 key\n\n用 `map()` 把数组转成组件数组，每项必须提供唯一 `key`（通常取数据库 ID），供 React 在插入/删除/重排时识别。\n\n```jsx\nconst products = [\n  { title: 'Cabbage', id: 1 },\n  { title: 'Garlic', id: 2 },\n  { title: 'Apple', id: 3 },\n];\n\nexport default function ShoppingList() {\n  const listItems = products.map(product => (\n    <li key={product.id}>{product.title}</li>\n  ));\n  return <ul>{listItems}</ul>;\n}\n```\n\n```jsx\nconst products = [\n  { title: 'Cabbage', isFruit: false, id: 1 },\n  { title: 'Garlic', isFruit: false, id: 2 },\n  { title: 'Apple', isFruit: true, id: 3 },\n];\n\nexport default function ShoppingList() {\n  return (\n    <ul>\n      {products.map(product => (\n        <li key={product.id} style={{ color: product.isFruit ? 'magenta' : 'darkgreen' }}>\n          {product.title}\n        </li>\n      ))}\n    </ul>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n## 8. Hooks 全景\n\n### 8.1 内置 Hooks 分类\n\n- **State Hooks**：`useState`、`useReducer`\n- **Effect Hooks**：`useEffect`、`useLayoutEffect`、`useInsertionEffect`\n- **Component Hooks**：`use`\n- **Library Hooks**：`useContext`、`useDebugValue`、`useMemo`、`useRef`、`useCallback`、`useId`、`useImperativeHandle`、`useSyncExternalStore`\n\n### 8.2 useEffect（副作用）\n\n```jsx\nimport { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\n\nfunction FriendStatus({ userId }) {\n  const [isOnline, setIsOnline] = useState(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    function startSubscription() {\n      const id = ChatServer.subscribeToFriendStatus(userId, isOnline =>\n        setIsOnline(isOnline)\n      );\n      return () => ChatServer.unsubscribeFromFriendStatus(userId, id);\n    }\n    return startSubscription();\n  }, [userId]);\n\n  return (\n    <span>{isOnline === null ? 'Loading...' : isOnline ? 'Online' : 'Offline'}</span>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n要点：\n- 副作用放 `useEffect`，**不要**放在渲染体里（渲染阶段应无副作用）。\n- 返回函数即\"清理/取消\"逻辑，在重新执行前与卸载时调用。\n- 依赖数组 `[userId]` 决定何时重新执行；省略则每次渲染都跑，`[]` 则只挂载一次。\n\n### 8.3 依赖数组与闭包陷阱\n\n```jsx\nimport { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\n\nfunction Profile({ userId }) {\n  const [age, setAge] = useState(0);\n\n  // 只在 userId 变化时重新拉取\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setAge(fetchAge(userId));\n  }, [userId]);\n\n  return <p>{userId} is {age}</p>;\n}\n```\n\n### 8.4 useReducer（复杂状态）\n\n```jsx\nimport { useReducer } from 'react';\n\nconst actions = { TICK: 'TICK' };\n\nfunction reducer(state, action) {\n  switch (action) {\n    case actions.TICK:\n      return state + 1;\n    default:\n      throw Error('Unknown action: ' + action);\n  }\n}\n\nexport function Ticker() {\n  const [count, dispatch] = useReducer(reducer, 0);\n  return (\n    <>\n      <p>Count: {count}</p>\n      <button onClick={() => dispatch(actions.TICK)}>+1</button>\n    </>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n### 8.5 useContext（跨层级共享数据）\n\n```jsx\nimport { createContext, useContext, useState } from 'react';\n\nconst ThemeContext = createContext('light');\n\nexport function Provider({ children }) {\n  const [theme, setTheme] = useState('light');\n  return (\n    <ThemeContext.Provider value={theme}>\n      {children}\n      <button onClick={() => setTheme(theme === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light')}>\n        toggle\n      </button>\n    </ThemeContext.Provider>\n  );\n}\n\nexport function Title() {\n  const theme = useContext(ThemeContext);\n  return <h1 style={{ color: theme }}>Themed Title</h1>;\n}\n```\n\n## 9. 自定义 Hook 与复用\n\n把 Hook 逻辑抽成 `useXxx` 函数，即可共享状态逻辑（普通逻辑则写普通工具函数）。\n\n```jsx\nimport { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\n\nexport function useFriendStatus(userId) {\n  const [isOnline, setIsOnline] = useState(null);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    function updateStatus() {\n      const isCurrentlyOnline = ChatServer.isFriendOnline(userId);\n      setIsOnline(isCurrentlyOnline);\n    }\n    updateStatus();\n    ChatServer.subscribeToFriendStatus(userId, updateStatus);\n    return () => ChatServer.unsubscribeFromFriendStatus(userId, updateStatus);\n  }, [userId]);\n  return isOnline;\n}\n\nfunction ChatMessage({ userId }) {\n  const isOnline = useFriendStatus(userId);\n  return <p>{isOnline ? 'Online' : 'Offline'}</p>;\n}\n```\n\n## 10. 数据流：状态提升与组合\n\n```jsx\nexport default function App() {\n  const [selected, setSelected] = useState('cabbage');\n  return (\n    <div>\n      <Product name=\"Cabbage\" selected={selected} onSelect={setSelected} />\n      <Product name=\"Apple\" selected={selected} onSelect={setSelected} />\n      <Cart selected={selected} />\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n原则：**单一数据源** + 向下传递 props，避免多份重复状态。\n\n## 11. 性能优化\n\n```jsx\nimport { memo, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';\n\nconst Row = memo(function Row({ item, onPick }) {\n  return (\n    <li onClick={() => onPick(item.id)}>\n      {item.title}\n    </li>\n  );\n});\n\nexport function List({ items }) {\n  const sorted = useMemo(() => [...items].sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id), [items]);\n  const handlePick = useCallback(id => console.log(id), []);\n  return sorted.map(i => <Row key={i.id} item={i} onPick={handlePick} />);\n}\n```\n\n## 12. 项目结构与工程化\n\n```bash\nnpm create vite@latest my-app -- --template react\ncd my-app\nnpm install\nnpm run dev\nnpm run build\n```\n\n```text\nsrc/\n  components/     # 可复用 UI 组件\n  features/       # 按业务域组织\n  hooks/          # 自定义 Hook\n  api/            # 数据请求\n  App.jsx         # 根组件\n  main.jsx        # createRoot 挂载入口\n```\n\n```jsx\nimport { StrictMode } from 'react';\nimport { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';\nimport App from './App.jsx';\n\ncreateRoot(document.getElementById('root')).render(\n  <StrictMode>\n    <App />\n  </StrictMode>\n);\n```\n\n## 13. 最佳实践清单\n\n- 组件名大写开头；一个文件一个主组件，`export default`。\n- 状态尽量下放到**最靠近使用处**的祖先组件，避免全局状态膨胀。\n- 列表必带稳定 `key`；不要在渲染体里修改 state。\n- 事件处理函数只传递不立即调用。\n- 副作用集中在 `useEffect`，并正确书写依赖数组与清理函数。\n- 优先函数组件 + Hooks；类组件仅用于兼容旧代码。\n- 遵循官方 Rules of React：Hooks 只可在组件/自定义 Hook 顶层调用。\n\n## 14. 推荐学习路径与资源\n\n1. **Quick Start**：react.dev/learn（覆盖日常 80% 概念）\n2. **官方教程**：react.dev/learn/tutorial（交互式，边改边看）\n3. **Hooks 参考**：react.dev/reference/react/hooks\n4. **React 规则**：react.dev/reference/rules/react-calls-components-and-hooks\n5. **JSX 参考**：react.dev/reference/react/jsx-transform\n6. 进阶：react.dev/learn/scaling-up-and-performance、react.dev/learn/managing-state\n7. 生态：路由 react-router、数据请求 TanStack Query、状态管理 Zustand、表单 React Hook Form\n"}`
**步骤5: write**
- 参数: `{"path": "E:\\test_dir\\go_manual.md", "content": "# Go 学习手册\n\n> 手册版本：v2.0 ｜ 生成时间：2026-10-04 14:12 ｜ 整理：SenseNova\n> 核心来源：Effective Go <https://go.dev/doc/effective_go>、Tour of Go、Go User Manual、Go Spec、go.dev/learn、go.dev/wiki/Learn\n> 官方定位：Go 追求**简洁、可靠、高效**，专为大规模软件构建而设计；直接照搬 Java/C++ 风格得不到好的 Go 代码。\n\n## 1. 设计理念\n\n- **格式化交给机器**：`gofmt` / `go fmt` 统一缩进与对齐，团队不争论风格。\n- **少即是多**：语法元素少（无类、无继承、无运算符重载）；控制语句不带括号。\n- **错误是值**：显式返回 `error`，不做异常机制。\n- **并发即语言特性**：goroutine 廉价，channel 通信，\"Don't communicate by sharing memory; share memory by communicating.\"\n- **包即单位**：包名小写单词、目录末段命名；导出靠**首字母大写**。\n\n## 2. 环境与 Hello World\n\n```bash\ngo version\ngo fmt ./...\ngo mod init example.com/hello\ngo run .\ngo test ./...\n```\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport \"fmt\"\n\nfunc main() {\n\tfmt.Println(\"Hello, Go\")\n}\n```\n\n> `main` 包 + `main()` 函数是程序入口；`fmt.Println` 是最常用的输出函数。\n\n## 3. 变量、常量与基本类型\n\n```go\n// 显式声明\nvar i int = 42\nvar f float64 = 3.14\nvar name string = \"Go\"\nvar ok bool = true\n\n// 短声明（函数内推荐）\nx := 1\ny := \"two\"\n\n// 常量：可用 const，支持 iota 枚举\nconst MaxRetry = 3\nconst (\n\ta = iota // 0\n\tb        // 1\n\tc        // 2\n)\n```\n\n```go\n// 类型转换：显式，无隐式转换\nvar n int = 42\nvar f float64 = float64(n)\nvar u uint = uint(f)\n\n// 字符串与字节\ns := \"hello\"\nb := []byte(s)\n```\n\n## 4. 复合字面量\n\n```go\nvar ints = []int{1, 2, 3, 4, 5}\nslice := []int{1, 2, 3}            // 省略元素类型\nm := map[string]int{\n\t\"one\": 1,\n\t\"two\": 2,\n}\n```\n\n```go\n// 结构体字面量\ntype Point struct {\n\tX, Y int\n}\n\np := Point{1, 2}      // 无序字面量（不推荐跨包使用）\nq := Point{X: 1, Y: 2} // 键值字面量（推荐）\nr := &Point{1, 2}     // 取地址得指针\n```\n\n## 5. 控制语句\n\nGo 语法：`if`/`for` 不带括号，条件不加括号，`{}` 必需，可带初始化语句。\n\n```go\nif v := f(); v < max {\n\tfmt.Println(v)\n} else if v == max {\n\tfmt.Println(\"max\")\n}\n```\n\n```go\n// for 是唯一的循环结构\nfor i := 0; i < 10; i++ {}\n\nn := 0\nfor n < 5 {\n\tn++\n}\n\nfor {\n\tbreak // 无限循环需显式 break\n}\n\nfor _, v := range []int{1, 2, 3} {\n\tfmt.Println(v)\n}\n\nfor i, c := range \"hello\" {\n\tfmt.Printf(\"%d %c\\n\", i, c) // 下标是 rune 偏移量\n}\n```\n\n```go\nswitch os.Args[0] {\ncase \"a\":\n\tfmt.Println(\"a\")\ncase \"b\", \"c\":\n\tfmt.Println(\"b or c\")\ndefault:\n\tfmt.Println(\"other\")\n}\n\nswitch x := f(); x.(type) {\ncase int:\n\tfmt.Println(\"int\")\ncase string:\n\tfmt.Println(\"string\")\ndefault:\n\tfmt.Println(\"unknown\")\n}\n```\n\n## 6. defer、panic 与 recover\n\n```go\nf, err := os.Open(\"config.txt\")\nif err != nil {\n\tlog.Fatal(err)\n}\ndefer f.Close() // 函数返回前按 LIFO 顺序执行\n```\n\n```go\nfunc divide(a, b int) (int, error) {\n\tif b == 0 {\n\t\tpanic(\"division by zero\")\n\t}\n\treturn a / b, nil\n}\n\nfunc safe() (result int) {\n\tdefer func() {\n\t\tif r := recover(); r != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Println(\"recovered:\", r)\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\tresult, _ = divide(10, 0)\n\treturn\n}\n```\n\n## 7. 数组、切片与 map\n\n```go\n// 数组长度固定，切片是可变窗口\nvar arr [3]int = [3]int{1, 2, 3}\ns := arr[:]\ns = append(s, 4)          // 追加\ns2 := s[1:3]              // 切片\ncopy(s2, s)               // 复制\nlen(s) / cap(s)           // 长度 / 容量\n```\n\n``` go\nm := map[string]string{\"k\": \"v\"}\nm[\"k2\"] = \"v2\"\nv, ok := m[\"k2\"]          // 双返回值：是否存在\ndelete(m, \"k\")\n```\n\n## 8. 指针与结构体方法\n\n```go\nfunc intPtr(v int) *int { return &v }\np := intPtr(5)\n*p = 6                    // 解引用后赋值\n```\n\n```go\ntype Rect struct {\n\tW, H int\n}\n\nfunc (r *Rect) Area() int { return r.W * r.H }   // 指针接收者\nfunc (r Rect) Set(w, h int)   { r.W, r.H = w, h } // 值接收者\n\nr := Rect{3, 4}\nr.Area()\n```\n\n> 方法接收者选择 `*T` 或 `T` 取决于是否需要修改接收者；同类型方法接收者必须一致。\n\n## 9. 函数、多返回值与错误\n\n```go\nfunc swap(a, b string) (string, string) {\n\treturn b, a\n}\n\nfirst, second := swap(\"x\", \"y\")\n```\n\n```go\nfunc doIt() (error) {\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nif err := doIt(); err != nil {\n\treturn err // 显式处理错误，不吞掉\n}\n```\n\n```go\n// 可变参数\nfunc sum(nums ...int) int {\n\ttotal := 0\n\tfor _, n := range nums {\n\t\ttotal += n\n\t}\n\treturn total\n}\nsum(1, 2, 3)\nsum([]int{1, 2, 3}...)\n```\n\n```go\n// 匿名函数与闭包\nf := func(a, b int) int { return a + b }\nf(1, 2)\n\nseq := func() func() int {\n\ti := 0\n\treturn func() int {\n\t\ti++\n\t\treturn i\n\t}\n}()\n```\n\n## 10. 接口（Interface）\n\n接口由方法集定义，实现是**隐式**的，无需 `implements` 关键字。\n\n```go\ntype Reader interface {\n\tRead(p []byte) (n int, err error)\n}\n\ntype Writer interface {\n\tWrite(p []byte) (n int, err error)\n}\n\ntype ReadWriter interface {\n\tReader\n\tWriter\n}\n```\n\n```go\ntype File struct{}\n\nfunc (File) Read(p []byte) (int, error)  { return 0, nil }\nfunc (File) Write(p []byte) (int, error) { return 0, nil }\n\nvar rw io.ReadWriter = File{} // 隐式实现\n```\n\n```go\n// any 与类型断言\nvar x any = \"hello\"\ns, isString := x.(string)\n```\n\n> Effective Go 约定：单方法接口用 \"-er\" 后缀命名（`Reader`/`Writer`/`Formatter`）；字符串转换方法叫 `String`，不叫 `ToString`；getter 不写 `Get` 前缀（`Owner()` 而非 `GetOwner()`）。\n\n## 11. 并发：goroutine 与 channel\n\n```go\n// goroutine\ngo fmt.Println(\"concurrent\")\ntime.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond) // 保证输出\n```\n\n```go\n// channel：发送、接收、阻塞\nch := make(chan int, 1)\nch <- 1        // 发送\nv := <-ch      // 接收\nn := len(ch)   // 元素数\nclosed := (ch == nil)\n```\n\n```go\n// 生产者 / 消费者 + select\njobs := make(chan int, 3)\ndone := make(chan bool)\n\nworker := func(id int) {\n\tfor j := range jobs {\n\t\tfmt.Println(id, \"got\", j)\n\t}\n\tdone <- true\n}\n\nfor i := 0; i < 3; i++ {\n\tgo worker(i)\n}\njobs <- 100\njobs <- 200\njobs <- 300\nclose(jobs)\n<-done\n```\n\n```go\n// channel 方向\nfunc sendOnly(ch chan<- int)  { ch <- 1 }\nfunc recvOnly(ch <-chan int) { _ = <---chan}\n```\n\n```go\n// select 多路复用\nc1, c2 := make(chan int), make(chan int)\ngo func() { c1 <- 1 }()\ngo func() { c2 <- 2 }()\n\nselect {\ncase v := <-c1:\n\tfmt.Println(\"from c1:\", v)\ncase v := <-c2:\n\tfmt.Println(\"from c2:\", v)\ncase <-time.After(50 * time.Millisecond):\n\tfmt.Println(\"timeout\")\n}\n```\n\n## 12. sync 与 context\n\n```go\nimport \"sync\"\n\nvar wg sync.WaitGroup\nfor i := 0; i < 5; i++ {\n\twg.Add(1)\n\tgo func(i int) {\n\t\tdefer wg.Done()\n\t\tfmt.Println(\"job\", i)\n\t}(i)\n}\nwg.Wait()\n```\n\n```go\nimport \"sync/atomic\"\n\nvar counter int64\natomic.AddInt64(&counter, 1)\natomic.LoadInt64(&counter)\n```\n\n```go\nctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Second)\ndefer cancel()\n\ngo func() {\n\tselect {\n\tcase <-time.After(5 * time.Second):\n\t\treturn\n\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\tfmt.Println(\"cancelled:\", ctx.Err())\n\t}\n}()\n<-ctx.Done()\n```\n\n## 13. 包、导入与项目结构\n\n```go\n// 标准导入\nimport \"fmt\"\n\n// 分组与别名\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\n\t\"github.com/gorilla/mux\"\n\talias \"golang.org/x/text/cases\"\n)\n```\n\n```text\nmymodule/\n  go.mod            # module 声明与依赖\n  go.sum\n  cmd/\n    server/main.go  # 可执行程序入口\n  internal/         # 仅本模块内可引用\n    auth/\n  pkg/ 或 api/      # 可复用库\n  testdata/\n```\n\n```bash\ngo mod init example.com/mymodule\ngo get github.com/gorilla/mux\ngo mod tidy\ngo build ./...\n```\n\n## 14. 测试\n\n```go\n// math_test.go\npackage math\n\nimport \"testing\"\n\nfunc TestAdd(t *testing.T) {\n\tif Add(1, 2) != 3 {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"Add(1,2) != 3\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc BenchmarkAdd(b *testing.B) {\n\tfor i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {\n\t\tAdd(1, 2)\n\t}\n}\n```\n\n```bash\ngo test -v ./...\ngo test -race ./...\n```\n\n## 15. 最佳实践清单（Effective Go 要点）\n\n- 用 **tab** 缩进；一律跑 `gofmt`，不要手工对齐。\n- 包名**小写单词**、无下划线、无 MixedCaps；构造器命名 `New`/`NewXxx`。\n- 导出标识符首字母大写；未导出的用小写，且不要留无用导出。\n- 注释规范：包级注释以包名开头；导出项写 doc comment；行注释为主。\n- 错误处理：`if err != nil` 是常态，包级哨兵错误用 `errors.New`，自定义错误实现 `error` 接口。\n- 并发：优先 channel 通信；用 `context` 传递取消与超时；用 `sync.WaitGroup` 汇聚。\n- 依赖管理统一使用 **Go Modules**（`go.mod`/`go.sum`），不要 `vendor` 除非必要。\n- 命名遵循 MixedCaps 而非 snake_case。\n\n## 16. 推荐学习路径与资源\n\n1. **Effective Go**：go.dev/doc/effective_go（清晰、地道写法指南）\n2. **Tour of Go**：go.dev/tour（分四部分的交互式入门）\n3. **Go 语言示例**：go.dev/doc/articles/examples（带注释的示例程序）\n4. **Go User Manual**：go.dev/doc/tutorial（完整介绍）\n5. **Go Spec**：go.dev/ref/spec（官方语言规范）\n6. **标准库文档**：pkg.go.dev\n7. **中文社区**：go-lang.org.cn、go.dev/wiki/Learn、go.dev/wiki/Training\n8. 进阶：《Programming in Go》（Donovan & Kernighan）、《Concurrency in Go》\n"}`

→ 1024\n-print("price:", 19.99)\n-```\n-\n----\n-\n-## 第 2 章 Python 速览：基本语法\n-\n-### 2.1 变量与动态类型\n-\n-```python\n-# 示例 2-1：变量绑定（不是类型）\n-name = "Ada"\n-age = 36\n-is_active = True\n-print(name, age, f"Dog({self.name}, { self.age})"\n+\n+d = Dog("Lucky", 3)\n+d.bark()\n+```\n+\n+```python\n+class Animal:\n+    def speak(self):\n+        return "..."\n+\n+class Cat(Animal):\n+    def speak(self):\n+        return "meow"\n+\n+for a in (Dog("Lucky", 3), Cat()):\n+    print(type(a).__name__, a.speak())   # 多态\n+```\n+\n+```python\n+from dataclasses import dataclass\n \n .venv/bin/activate\n-pip install --upgrade pip\n-pip install requests flask\n-pip freeze > requirements.txt\n-```\n-\n----\n-\n-## 第 12 章 学习路径与推荐资源\n-\n-1. **官方教程**：https://docs.python.org/zh-cn/3/tutorial/ （本手册结构基础）\n-2. **语言参考手册**：https://docs.python.org/zh-cn/3/reference/\n-3. **标准库参考**：https://docs.python.org/zh-cn/3/library/\n-4. **PEP 文档**：https://peps.python.org/ （语言演进提案，如 PEP 8 风格指南）\n-5. **Python 官网**：https://www.python.org/\n-6. **经典书籍**：《流畅的 Python》《Python 编程：快速上手》\n-\n-## 附录 A 术语对照表（节选）\n-\n-| 英文 | 中文 |\n-|------|------|\n-| module | 模块 |\n-| package | 包 |\n-| attribute | 属性 |\n-| call | 调用 |\n-| class | 类 |\n-| function | 函数 |\n-| method | 方法 |\n-| yield | 生成 |\n-| iterator | 迭代器 |\n+class Point:\n+    x: float\n+    y: float\n+    def dist(self):\n+        return (self.x ** 2 + self.y ** 2) ** 0.5\n+\n+p = Point(3, 4)\n+p.dist()\n+```\n+\n+### 7.1 上下文管理器\n+\n+```python\n+class File:\n+    def __enter__(self):\n+        return self\n+\n+    def __exit__(self, *exc):\n+        print("closed")\n+\n+with File() as f:\n+    pass\n+```\n+\n+## 8. 异常处理\n+\n+```python\n+try:\n+    v = 1 / 0\n+except ZeroDivisionError as e:\n+    print("ZeroDivisionError:", e)\n+except Exception as e:\n+    print("other:", e)\n+else:\n+    print("no error")\n+finally:\n+    print("always")\n+```\n+\n+```python\n+class BizError(Exception):\n+    def __init__(self, code, msg):\n+        super().__init__(msg)\n+        self.code = code\n+\n+try:\n+    raise BizError(404, "not found")\n+except BizError as e:\n+    print(e.code, e)\n+```\n+\n+## 9. 文件、模块与标准库\n+\n+```python\n+with open("a.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:\n+    f.write("hello\\n")\n+\n+with open("a.txt", encoding="utf-8") as f:\n+    for line in f:\n+        print(line.strip())\n+```\n+\n+```python\n+import json, csv, os, re, sys, datetime\n+from collections import defaultdict, Counter\n+from pathlib import Path\n+\n+d = {"a": 1}\n+json.dumps(d, ensure_ascii=False)\n+json.loads(\'{"a": 1}\')\n+\n+Counter("aab").most_common(1)\n+re.findall(r"\\d+", "a1b2")\n+datetime.datetime.now().isoformat()\n+```\n+\n+```python\n+# 导入：from module import func / import module as m\n+import urllib.request\n+urllib.request.urlopen("https://python.org").status\n+```\n+\n+## 10. 类型注解\n+\n+```python\n+from typing import List, Optional\n+\n+def greet(names: List[str], times: int = 1) -> None:\n+    for n in names:\n+        print(n, times)\n+\n+def find(xs: list[int], key: str) -> Optional[str]:\n+    return next((x for x in xs if key in x), None)\n+```\n+\n+## 11. 日志与并发\n+\n+```python\n+import logging\n+logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s")\n+log = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n+log.info("started")\n+```\n+\n+```python\n+from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\n+import urllib.request\n+\n+def fetch(url):\n+    return urllib.request.urlopen(url).status\n+\n+with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as ex:\n+    results = list(ex.map(fetch, ["https://python.org"] * 3))\n+```\n+\n+## 12. 最佳实践（PEP 8 要点）\n+\n+| 规则 | 建议 |\n+|---|---|\n+| 缩进 | 4 个空格，不用 Tab |\n+| 行宽 | 建议 ≤ 79 字符 |\n+| 命名 | 变量/函数 `snake_case`，类 `CamelCase`，常量 `UPPER_CASE` |\n+| 导入 | 一行一个模块，`import os` 而非 `from os import *` |\n+| 空格 | 运算符两侧、逗号后加空格 |\n+| 文档 | 模块/函数写 docstring |\n+| 工具 | 用 `ruff` / `black` / `mypy` 统一格式与类型检查 |\n+\n+## 13. 推荐学习路径与资源\n+\n+1. **官方教程**：docs.python.org/zh-cn/3/tutorial/（循序渐进，示例完备自足）\n+2. self.age})\\"\\n+\\n+d = Dog(\\"Lucky\\", 3)\\n+d.bark()\\n+```\\n+\\n+```python\\n+class Animal:\\n+    def speak(self):\\n+        return \\"...\\"\\n+\\n+class Cat(Animal):\\n+    def |\\n-|------|------|\\n-| module | 模块 |\\n-| package | 包 |\\n-| attribute | 属性 |\\n-| call | 调用 |\\n-| class | 类 |\\n-| function | 函数 |\\n-| method | 方法 |\\n-| yield | 生成 |\\n-| iterator | 迭代器 |\\n+class Point:\\n+    x: float\\n+    y: float\\n+    def dist(self):\\n+        return (self.x ** 2 + self.y ** 2) ** 0.5\\n+\\n+p = Point(3, 4)\\n+p.dist()\\n+```\\n+\\n+### 7.1 上下文管理器\\n+\\n+```python\\n+class File:\\n+    def __enter__(self):\\n+        return self\\n+\\n+    def __exit__(self, *exc):\\n+        print(\\"closed\\")\\n+\\n+with File() as f:\\n+    pass\\n+```\\n+\\n+## 8. 异常处理\\n+\\n+```python\\n+try:\\n+    v = 1 / 0\\n+except ZeroDivisionError as e:\\n+    print(\\"ZeroDivisionError:\\", e)\\n+except Exception as e:\\n+    print(\\"other:\\", e)\\n+else:\\n+    print(\\"no error\\")\\n+finally:\\n+    print(\\"always\\")\\n+```\\n+\\n+```python\\n+class BizError(Exception):\\n+    def __init__(self, code, msg):\\n+        super().__init__(msg)\\n+        self.code = code\\n+\\n+try:\\n+    raise BizError(404, \\"not found\\")\\n+except BizError as e:\\n+    print(e.code, e)\\n+```\\n+\\n+## 9. 文件、模块与标准库\\n+\\n+```python\\n+with open(\\"a.txt\\", \\"w\\", encoding=\\"utf-8\\") as f:\\n+    f.write(\\"hello\\\\n\\")\\n+\\n+with open(\\"a.txt\\", encoding=\\"utf-8\\") as f:\\n+    for line in f:\\n+        print(line.strip())\\n+```\\n+\\n+```python\\n+import json, csv, os, re, sys, datetime\\n+from collections import defaultdict, Counter\\n+from pathlib import Path\\n+\\n+d = {\\"a\\": 1}\\n+json.dumps(d, ensure_ascii=False)\\n+json.loads(\'{\\"a\\": 1}\')\\n+\\n+Counter(\\"aab\\").most_common(1)\\n+re.findall(r\\"\\\\d+\\", \\"a1b2\\")\\n+datetime.datetime.now().isoformat()\\n+```\\n+\\n+```python\\n+# 导入：from module import func / import module as m\\n+import urllib.request\\n+urllib.request.urlopen(\\"https://python.org\\").status\\n+```\\n+\\n+## 10. 类型注解\\n+\\n+```python\\n+from typing import List, Optional\\n+\\n+def greet(names: List[str], times: int = 1) -> None:\\n+    for n in names:\\n+        print(n, times)\\n+\\n+def find(xs: list[int], key: str) -> Optional[str]:\\n+    return next((x for x in xs if key in x), None)\\n+```\\n+\\n+## 11. 日志与并发\\n+\\n+```python\\n+import logging\\n+logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\")\\n+log = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n+log.info(\\"started\\")\\n+```\\n+\\n+```python\\n+from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\\n+import urllib.request\\n+\\n+def fetch(url):\\n+    return urllib.request.urlopen(url).status\\n+\\n+with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as ex:\\n+    results = list(ex.map(fetch, [\\"https://python.org\\"] * 3))\\n+```\\n+\\n+## 12. 最佳实践（PEP 8 要点）\\n+\\n+| 规则 | 建议 |\\n+|---|---|\\n+| 缩进 | 4 个空格，不用 Tab |\\n+| 行宽 | 建议 ≤ 79 字符 |\\n+| 命名 | 变量/函数 `snake_case`，类 `CamelCase`，常量 `UPPER_CASE` |\\n+| 导入 | 一行一个模块，`import os` 而非 `from os import *` |\\n+| 空格 | 运算符两侧、逗号后加空格 |\\n+| 文档 | 模块/函数写 docstring |\\n+| 工具 | 用 `ruff` / `black` / `mypy` 统一格式与类型检查 |\\n+\\n+## 13. 推荐学习路径与资源\\n+\\n+1. 第 1 章 React 概览\n-\n-React 是用于构建用户界面的 JavaScript 库，核心思想是**声明式 UI**：描述界面"应该是什么样"，而非"如何一步步变成那样"。\n-\n-**核心支柱**：\n-\n-| 概念 | 说明 |\n-|------|------|\n-| Components（组件） | 最小 UI 单元，可组合复用 |\n-| Elements（元素） | UI 的最小描述对象 |\n-| Props（属性） | 父向子的只读数据传递 |\n-| State（状态） | 组件内随时间变化的数据 |\n-| Hooks（钩子） | 复用状态逻辑与副作用 |\n-| JSX | 在 JS 中书写类 HTML 标记 |\n-\n-```jsx\n-// 示例 1-1：第一个 React 元素与渲染\n-import { createRoot } from \'react-dom/client\';\n-\n-const element = <h1>Hello, React</h1>;\n-const root = createRoot(document.getElementById(\'root\'));\n-root.render(element);\n-```\n-\n----\n-\n-## 第 2 章 JSX 深入\n-\n-JSX 是 JavaScript 的语法扩展，编译后变成 `React.createElement` 调用。\n-\n-```jsx\n-// 示例 2-1：JSX 基本规则\n-const name = \'Lin\';\n-const output = (\n-  <div className="greeting">\n-    <h1>你好，{name}！</h1>\n-    {1 + 1}\n-  </div>\n-);\n-// 编译结果：React.createElement(\'div\', { className: \'greeting\' }, ...)\n-```\n-\n-```jsx\n-// 示例 2-2：表达式与模板字符串\n-const first = \'Ada\';\n-const last = \'Lovelace\';\n-const msg = `Hello, ${first} ${last}`;\n-return <p>{msg}</p>;\n-```\n-\n-```jsx\n-// 示例 2-3：渲染列表与 key\n-const items = [\'apple\', \'banana\', \'cherry\'];\n-return (\n-  <ul>\n-    {items.map((item, i) => (\n-      <li key={item}>{item}</li>\n-    ))}\n-  </ul>\n-);\n-```\n-\n-```jsx\n-// 示例 2-4：条件渲染\n-function Banner({ isAdmin }) {\n-  return isAdmin ? <div className="admin">管理员</div> : <div className="user">访客</div>;\n-}\n-```\n-\n----\n-\n-## 第 3 章 组件与 Props\n-\n-### 3.1 组件是普通函数\n-\n-```jsx\n-// 示例 3-1：函数组件\n-function Welcome({ name }) {\n-  return <h1>你好，{name}</h1>;\n+> 手册版本：v2.0 ｜ 生成时间：2026-10-04 14:12 ｜ 整理：SenseNova\n+> 核心来源：React 官方文档 <https://react.dev/learn>（Quick Start 覆盖 80% 日常概念）、react.dev/reference/react/hooks、React 规则（Rules of React）\n+> 当前主版本：React 19（19.1 系列）\n+\n+## 1. 心智模型\n+\n+- React 应用由**组件（Component）**组成：组件是拥有自身逻辑与外观的 UI 片段，可小到按钮、大到整页。\n+- 组件就是**返回 markup 的 JavaScript 函数**。\n+- React 是**声明式**的：你描述"要渲染什么"，React 决定"如何显示"。\n+- 组件与 Hooks 只能由 React 调用（在 JSX 中），**不要像普通函数那样直接调用组件**。\n+\n+## 2. 组件与 JSX\n+\n+```jsx\n+function MyButton() {\n+  return (\n+    <button>I\'m a button</button>\n+  );\n+}\n+```\n+\n+```jsx\n+export default function MyApp() {\n+  return (\n+    <div>\n+      <h1>Welcome to my app</h1>\n+      <MyButton />\n+    </div>\n+  );\n+}\n+```\n+\n+> 约定：组件名必须**大写开头**（`<MyButton />`），HTML 标签小写。`export default` 指定文件主组件。\n+\n+### 2.1 JSX 语法约束\n+\n+JSX 比 HTML 更严格：自闭合标签要写 `<br />`；返回的 JSX 必须有单一父节点（`<div>` 或空片段 `<>...</>`）。\n+\n+```jsx\n+function AboutPage() {\n+  return (\n+    <>\n+      <h1>About</h1>\n+      <p>Hello there.<br />How do you do?</p>\n+    </>\n+  );\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 2.2 样式\n+\n+React 中 CSS 类名用 `className`，规则写在单独的 CSS 文件里；依赖 JS 变量时用 `style`。\n+\n+```css\n+/* In your CSS */\n+.avatar {\n+  border-radius: 50%;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+```jsx\n+const user = {\n+  name: \'Hedy Lamarr\',\n+  imageUrl: \'https://react.dev/images/docs/scientists/yXOvdOSs.jpg\',\n+  imageSize: 90,\n+};\n+\n+export default function Profile() {\n+  return (\n+    <>\n+      <h1>{user.name}</h1>\n+      <img\n+        className="avatar"\n+        src={user.imageUrl}\n+        alt={\'Photo of \' + user.name}\n+        style={{ width: user.imageSize, height: user.imageSize }}\n+      />\n+    </>\n+  );\n+}\n+```\n+\n+> JSX 中用 `{}` 插值 JS 表达式；属性里用 `{}` 而**不是**引号：`className="avatar"` 传字符串，`src={user.imageUrl}` 读变量。\n+\n+## 3. Props 与数据传递\n+\n+```jsx\n+function Greeting({ name, role }) {\n+  return <p>{role} {name}</p>;\n }\n \n export default function App() {\n-  return <Welcome name="World" />;\n-}\n-```\n-\n-### 3.2 组件即组件\n-\n-```jsx\n-// 示例 3-2：组件组合（复用）\n-function Row({ children }) {\n-  return <tr>{children}</tr>;\n-}\n-\n-function Table() {\n-  return (\n-    <table>\n-      <tbody>\n-        <Row><td>1</td><td>Ada</td></Row>\n-        <Row><td>2</td><td>Grace</td></Row>\n-      </tbody>\n-    </table>\n-  );\n-}\n-```\n-\n-### 3.3 children 与条件渲染\n-\n-```jsx\n-// 示例 3-3：children\n-function Card({ title, children }) {\n-  return (\n-    <section>\n-      <h2>{title}</h2>\n+  return <Greeting name="Ada" role="Engineer" />;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+```jsx\n+function Card({ children }) {\n+  return <section className="card">{children}</section>;\n+}\n+\n+<Card>\n+  <h3>Profile</h3>\n+  <p>Children prop 承载任意子节点</p>\n+</Card>\n+```\n+\n+## 4. State 与 useState\n+\n+React 没有状态就无法"记住"信息。用 `useState` 为函数组件添加本地状态，React 会在多次渲染之间**保留该状态**。\n+\n+```jsx\n+import { useState } from \'react\';\n+\n+function MyButton() {\n+  const [count, setCount] = useState(0);\n+  return (\n+    <button onClick={() => setCount(count + 1)}>\n+      You clicked {count} times\n+    </button>\n+  );\n+}\n+```\n+\n+要点：\n+- `useState` 返回一对值：当前状态 + 更新函数。\n+- 更新状态后 **React 重渲染**，所以更新逻辑必须放在事件处理函数里，不能放在渲染体中直接改。\n+- 传入与当前值相同的值时，React 用 `Object.is` 比较后跳过子渲染。\n+\n+```jsx\n+import { useState } from \'react\';\n+\n+export default function Form() {\n+  const [name, setName] = useState(\'\');\n+  return (\n+    <form>\n+      <label>\n+        Name:{\' \'}\n+        <input value={name} onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)} />\n+      </label>\n+      <p>Hello, {name || \'stranger\'}!</p>\n+    </form>\n+  );\n+}\n+```\n+\n+> 受控组件：`value` + `onChange` 让输入框完全由 state 驱动。\n+\n+## 5. 事件处理\n+\n+```jsx\n+function MyButton() {\n+  function handleClick() {\n+    alert(\'You clicked me!\');\n+  }\n+  return (\n+    <button onClick={handleClick}>Click me</button>\n+  );\n+}\n+```\n+\n+> `onClick={handleClick}` **末尾没有括号**：只传递函数，不要调用它；React 会在点击时调用。\n+\n+## 6. 条件渲染\n+\n+React 没有专门的条件语法，直接用 JavaScript：`if...else`、三元 `? :`、逻辑与 `&&`。\n+\n+```jsx\n+let content;\n+if (isLoggedIn) {\n+  content = <AdminPanel />;\n+} else {\n+  content = <LoginForm />;\n+}\n+return <div>{content}</div>;\n+```\n+\n+```jsx\n+<div>\n+  {isLoggedIn ? <AdminPanel /> : <LoginForm />}\n+  {isFavorite && <FavoriteIcon />}\n+</div>\n+```\n+\n+## 7. 列表渲染与 key\n+\n+用 `map()` 把数组转成组件数组，每项必须提供唯一 `key`（通常取数据库 ID），供 React 在插入/删除/重排时识别。\n+\n+```jsx\n+const products = [\n+  { title: \'Cabbage\', id: 1 },\n+  { title: \'Garlic\', id: 2 },\n+  { title: \'Apple\', id: 3 },\n+];\n+\n+export default function ShoppingList() {\n+  const listItems = products.map(product => (\n+    <li key={product.id}>{product.title}</li>\n+  ));\n+  return <ul>{listItems}</ul>;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+```jsx\n+const products = [\n+  { title: \'Cabbage\', isFruit: false, id: 1 },\n+  { title: \'Garlic\', isFruit: false, id: 2 },\n+  { title: \'Apple\', isFruit: true, id: 3 },\n+];\n+\n+export default function ShoppingList() {\n+  return (\n+    <ul>\n+      {products.map(product => (\n+        <li key={product.id} style={{ color: product.isFruit ? \'magenta\' : \'darkgreen\' }}>\n+          {product.title}\n+        </li>\n+      ))}\n+    </ul>\n+  );\n+}\n+```\n+\n+## 8. Hooks 全景\n+\n+### 8.1 内置 Hooks 分类\n+\n+- **State Hooks**：`useState`、`useReducer`\n+- **Effect Hooks**：`useEffect`、`useLayoutEffect`、`useInsertionEffect`\n+- **Component Hooks**：`use`\n+- **Library Hooks**：`useContext`、`useDebugValue`、`useMemo`、`useRef`、`useCallback`、`useId`、`useImperativeHandle`、`useSyncExternalStore`\n+\n+### 8.2 useEffect（副作用）\n+\n+```jsx\n+import { useEffect, useState } from \'react\';\n+\n+function FriendStatus({ userId }) {\n+  const [isOnline, setIsOnline] = useState(null);\n+\n+  useEffect(() => {\n+    function startSubscription() {\n+      const id = ChatServer.subscribeToFriendStatus(userId, isOnline =>\n+        setIsOnline(isOnline)\n+      );\n+      return () => ChatServer.unsubscribeFromFriendStatus(userId, id);\n+    }\n+    return startSubscription();\n+  }, [userId]);\n+\n+  return (\n+    <span>{isOnline === null ? \'Loading...\' : isOnline ? \'Online\' : \'Offline\'}</span>\n+  );\n+}\n+```\n+\n+要点：\n+- 副作用放 `useEffect`，**不要**放在渲染体里（渲染阶段应无副作用）。\n+- 返回函数即"清理/取消"逻辑，在重新执行前与卸载时调用。\n+- 依赖数组 `[userId]` 决定何时重新执行；省略则每次渲染都跑，`[]` 则只挂载一次。\n+\n+### 8.3 依赖数组与闭包陷阱\n+\n+```jsx\n+import { useEffect, useState } from \'react\';\n+\n+function Profile({ userId }) {\n+  const [age, setAge] = useState(0);\n+\n+  // 只在 userId 变化时重新拉取\n+  useEffect(() => {\n+    setAge(fetchAge(userId));\n+  }, [userId]);\n+\n+  return <p>{userId} is {age}</p>;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 8.4 useReducer（复杂状态）\n+\n+```jsx\n+import { useReducer } from \'react\';\n+\n+const actions = { TICK: \'TICK\' };\n+\n+function reducer(state, action) {\n+  switch (action) {\n+    case actions.TICK:\n+      return state + 1;\n+    default:\n+      throw Error(\'Unknown action: \' + action);\n+  }\n+}\n+\n+export function Ticker() {\n+  const [count, dispatch] = useReducer(reducer, 0);\n+  return (\n+    <>\n+      <p>Count: {count}</p>\n+      <button onClick={() => dispatch(actions.TICK)}>+1</button>\n+    </>\n+  );\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 8.5 src/\n-│  ├─ main.jsx          # 入口\n-│  ├─ App.jsx           # 根组件\n-│  ├─ components/       # 通用组件\n-│  ├─ features/         # 业务模块\n-│  ├─ hooks/            # 自定义 Hook\n-│  ├─ api/              # React（https://react-typescript-cheatsheet.netlify.app/）\n-\n-## 附录 A 常用 Hooks 速查\n-\n-| Hook | 签名 | 返回 |\n-|------|------|------|\n-| useState | `useState(initial)` | `[value, setValue]` |\n-| useEffect | `useEffect(fn, deps?)` | `void` |\n-| useReducer | `useReducer(reducer, init)` | `[state, dispatch]` |\n-| useRef | `useRef(initial)` | `{ current }` |\n-| useContext | `useContext(Ctx)` | 上下文值 |\n-| useMemo | `useMemo(fn, deps)` | 计算结果 |\n-| useCallback | `useCallback(fn, deps)` | 稳定函数引用 |\n-| useId | `useId()` | 稳定字符串 id |\n+npm run build\n+```\n+\n+```text\n+src/\n+  components/     # 可复用 UI 组件\n+  features/       # 按业务域组织\n+  hooks/          # 自定义 Hook\n+  api/            # 数据请求\n+  App.jsx         # 根组件\n+  main.jsx        # createRoot 挂载入口\n+```\n+\n+```jsx\n+import { StrictMode } from \'react\';\n+import { createRoot } from \'react-dom/client\';\n+import App from \'./App.jsx\';\n+\n+createRoot(document.getElementById(\'root\')).render(\n+  <StrictMode>\n+    <App />\n+  </StrictMode>\n+);\n+```\n+\n+## 13. 最佳实践清单\n+\n+- 组件名大写开头；一个文件一个主组件，`export default`。\n+- 状态尽量下放到**最靠近使用处**的祖先组件，避免全局状态膨胀。\n+- 列表必带稳定 `key`；不要在渲染体里修改 state。\n+- 事件处理函数只传递不立即调用。\n+- 副作用集中在 `useEffect`，并正确书写依赖数组与清理函数。\n+- 优先函数组件 + Hooks；类组件仅用于兼容旧代码。\n+- 遵循官方 Rules of React：Hooks 只可在组件/自定义 Hook 顶层调用。\n+\n+## 14. 推荐学习路径与资源\n+\n+1. **Quick Start**：react.dev/learn（覆盖日常 80% 概念）\n+2. **官方教程**：react.dev/learn/tutorial（交互式，边改边看）\n+3. **Hooks 参考**：react.dev/reference/react/hooks\n+4. **React 规则**：react.dev/reference/rules/react-calls-components-and-hooks\n+5. **JSX 参考**：react.dev/reference/react/jsx-transform\n+6. 进阶：react.dev/learn/scaling-up-and-performance、react.dev/learn/managing-state\n+7. 生态：路由 react-router、数据请求 TanStack Query、状态管理 Zustand、表单 React Hook UI**：描述界面\\"应该是什么样\\"，而非\\"如何一步步变成那样\\"。\\n-\\n-**核心支柱**：\\n-\\n-| 概念 | 说明 |\\n-|------|------|\\n-| Components（组件） | 最小 UI 单元，可组合复用 |\\n-| Elements（元素） | UI 的最小描述对象 |\\n-| Props（属性） | 父向子的只读数据传递 |\\n-| State（状态） | 组件内随时间变化的数据 |\\n-| Hooks（钩子） | 复用状态逻辑与副作用 |\\n-| JSX | 在 JS 中书写类 HTML 标记 |\\n-\\n-```jsx\\n-// 示例 1-1：第一个 React 元素与渲染\\n-import { createRoot } from \'react-dom/client\';\\n-\\n-const element = <h1>Hello, React</h1>;\\n-const root = createRoot(document.getElementById(\'root\'));\\n-root.render(element);\\n-```\\n-\\n----\\n-\\n-## 第 2 章 JSX 深入\\n-\\n-JSX 是 JavaScript 的语法扩展，编译后变成 `React.createElement` 调用。\\n-\\n-```jsx\\n-// 示例 2-1：JSX 基本规则\\n-const name = \'Lin\';\\n-const output = (\\n-  <div className=\\"greeting\\">\\n-    <h1>你好，{name}！</h1>\\n-    {1 + 1}\\n-  </div>\\n-);\\n-// 编译结果：React.createElement(\'div\', { className: \'greeting\' }, ...)\\n-```\\n-\\n-```jsx\\n-// 示例 2-2：表达式与模板字符串\\n-const first = \'Ada\';\\n-const last = \'Lovelace\';\\n-const msg = `Hello, ${first} ${last}`;\\n-return <p>{msg}</p>;\\n-```\\n-\\n-```jsx\\n-// 示例 2-3：渲染列表与 key\\n-const items = [\'apple\', \'banana\', \'cherry\'];\\n-return (\\n-  <ul>\\n-    {items.map((item, i) => (\\n-      <li key={item}>{item}</li>\\n-    ))}\\n-  </ul>\\n-);\\n-```\\n-\\n-```jsx\\n-// 示例 2-4：条件渲染\\n-function Banner({ isAdmin }) {\\n-  return isAdmin ? <div className=\\"admin\\">管理员</div> : <div className=\\"user\\">访客</div>;\\n-}\\n-```\\n-\\n----\\n-\\n-## 第 3 章 组件与 Props\\n-\\n-### 3.1 组件是普通函数\\n-\\n-```jsx\\n-// 示例 3-1：函数组件\\n-function Welcome({ name }) {\\n-  return <h1>你好，{name}</h1>;\\n+> 手册版本：v2.0 ｜ 生成时间：2026-10-04 14:12 ｜ 整理：SenseNova\\n+> 核心来源：React 官方文档 <https://react.dev/learn>（Quick Start 覆盖 80% 日常概念）、react.dev/reference/react/hooks、React 规则（Rules of React）\\n+> 当前主版本：React 19（19.1 系列）\\n+\\n+## 1. 心智模型\\n+\\n+- React 应用由**组件（Component）**组成：组件是拥有自身逻辑与外观的 UI 片段，可小到按钮、大到整页。\\n+- 组件就是**返回 markup 的 JavaScript 函数**。\\n+- React 是**声明式**的：你描述\\"要渲染什么\\"，React 决定\\"如何显示\\"。\\n+- 组件与 Hooks 只能由 React 调用（在 JSX 中），**不要像普通函数那样直接调用组件**。\\n+\\n+## 2. 组件与 JSX\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+function MyButton() {\\n+  return (\\n+    <button>I\'m a button</button>\\n+  );\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+export default function MyApp() {\\n+  return (\\n+    <div>\\n+      <h1>Welcome to my app</h1>\\n+      <MyButton />\\n+    </div>\\n+  );\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+> 约定：组件名必须**大写开头**（`<MyButton />`），HTML 标签小写。`export default` 指定文件主组件。\\n+\\n+### 2.1 JSX 语法约束\\n+\\n+JSX 比 HTML 更严格：自闭合标签要写 `<br />`；返回的 JSX 必须有单一父节点（`<div>` 或空片段 `<>...</>`）。\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+function AboutPage() {\\n+  return (\\n+    <>\\n+      <h1>About</h1>\\n+      <p>Hello there.<br />How do you do?</p>\\n+    </>\\n+  );\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+### 2.2 样式\\n+\\n+React 中 CSS 类名用 `className`，规则写在单独的 CSS 文件里；依赖 JS 变量时用 `style`。\\n+\\n+```css\\n+/* In your CSS */\\n+.avatar {\\n+  border-radius: 50%;\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+const user = {\\n+  name: \'Hedy Lamarr\',\\n+  imageUrl: \'https://react.dev/images/docs/scientists/yXOvdOSs.jpg\',\\n+  imageSize: 90,\\n+};\\n+\\n+export default function Profile() {\\n+  return (\\n+    <>\\n+      <h1>{user.name}</h1>\\n+      <img\\n+        className=\\"avatar\\"\\n+        src={user.imageUrl}\\n+        alt={\'Photo of \' + user.name}\\n+        style={{ width: user.imageSize, height: user.imageSize }}\\n+      />\\n+    </>\\n+  );\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+> JSX 中用 `{}` 插值 JS 表达式；属性里用 `{}` 而**不是**引号：`className=\\"avatar\\"` 传字符串，`src={user.imageUrl}` 读变量。\\n+\\n+## 3. Props 与数据传递\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+function Greeting({ name, role }) {\\n+  return <p>{role} {name}</p>;\\n }\\n \\n export default function App() {\\n-  return <Welcome name=\\"World\\" />;\\n-}\\n-```\\n-\\n-### 3.2 组件即组件\\n-\\n-```jsx\\n-// 示例 3-2：组件组合（复用）\\n-function Row({ children }) {\\n-  return <tr>{children}</tr>;\\n-}\\n-\\n-function Table() {\\n-  return (\\n-    <table>\\n-      <tbody>\\n-        <Row><td>1</td><td>Ada</td></Row>\\n-        <Row><td>2</td><td>Grace</td></Row>\\n-      </tbody>\\n-    </table>\\n-  );\\n-}\\n-```\\n-\\n-### 3.3 children 与条件渲染\\n-\\n-```jsx\\n-// 示例 3-3：children\\n-function Card({ title, children }) {\\n-  return (\\n-    <section>\\n-      <h2>{title}</h2>\\n+  return <Greeting name=\\"Ada\\" role=\\"Engineer\\" />;\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+function Card({ children }) {\\n+  return <section className=\\"card\\">{children}</section>;\\n+}\\n+\\n+<Card>\\n+  <h3>Profile</h3>\\n+  <p>Children prop 承载任意子节点</p>\\n+</Card>\\n+```\\n+\\n+## 4. State 与 useState\\n+\\n+React 没有状态就无法\\"记住\\"信息。用 `useState` 为函数组件添加本地状态，React 会在多次渲染之间**保留该状态**。\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+import { useState } from \'react\';\\n+\\n+function MyButton() {\\n+  const [count, setCount] = useState(0);\\n+  return (\\n+    <button onClick={() => setCount(count + 1)}>\\n+      You clicked {count} times\\n+    </button>\\n+  );\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+要点：\\n+- `useState` 返回一对值：当前状态 + 更新函数。\\n+- 更新状态后 **React 重渲染**，所以更新逻辑必须放在事件处理函数里，不能放在渲染体中直接改。\\n+- 传入与当前值相同的值时，React 用 `Object.is` 比较后跳过子渲染。\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+import { useState } from \'react\';\\n+\\n+export default function Form() {\\n+  const [name, setName] = useState(\'\');\\n+  return (\\n+    <form>\\n+      <label>\\n+        Name:{\' \'}\\n+        <input value={name} onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)} />\\n+      </label>\\n+      <p>Hello, {name || \'stranger\'}!</p>\\n+    </form>\\n+  );\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+> 受控组件：`value` + `onChange` 让输入框完全由 state 驱动。\\n+\\n+## 5. 事件处理\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+function MyButton() {\\n+  function handleClick() {\\n+    alert(\'You clicked me!\');\\n+  }\\n+  return (\\n+    <button onClick={handleClick}>Click me</button>\\n+  );\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+> `onClick={handleClick}` **末尾没有括号**：只传递函数，不要调用它；React 会在点击时调用。\\n+\\n+## 6. 条件渲染\\n+\\n+React 没有专门的条件语法，直接用 JavaScript：`if...else`、三元 `? :`、逻辑与 `&&`。\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+let content;\\n+if (isLoggedIn) {\\n+  content = <AdminPanel />;\\n+} else {\\n+  content = <LoginForm />;\\n+}\\n+return <div>{content}</div>;\\n+```\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+<div>\\n+  {isLoggedIn ? <AdminPanel /> : <LoginForm />}\\n+  {isFavorite && <FavoriteIcon />}\\n+</div>\\n+```\\n+\\n+## 7. 列表渲染与 key\\n+\\n+用 `map()` 把数组转成组件数组，每项必须提供唯一 `key`（通常取数据库 ID），供 React 在插入/删除/重排时识别。\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+const products = [\\n+  { title: \'Cabbage\', id: 1 },\\n+  { title: \'Garlic\', id: 2 },\\n+  { title: \'Apple\', id: 3 },\\n+];\\n+\\n+export default function ShoppingList() {\\n+  const listItems = products.map(product => (\\n+    <li key={product.id}>{product.title}</li>\\n+  ));\\n+  return <ul>{listItems}</ul>;\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+const products = [\\n+  { title: \'Cabbage\', isFruit: false, id: 1 },\\n+  { title: \'Garlic\', isFruit: false, id: 2 },\\n+  { title: \'Apple\', isFruit: true, id: 3 },\\n+];\\n+\\n+export default function ShoppingList() {\\n+  return (\\n+    <ul>\\n+      {products.map(product => (\\n+        <li key={product.id} style={{ color: product.isFruit ? \'magenta\' : \'darkgreen\' }}>\\n+          {product.title}\\n+        </li>\\n+      ))}\\n+    </ul>\\n+  );\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+## 8. Hooks 全景\\n+\\n+### 8.1 内置 Hooks 分类\\n+\\n+- **State Hooks**：`useState`、`useReducer`\\n+- **Effect Hooks**：`useEffect`、`useLayoutEffect`、`useInsertionEffect`\\n+- **Component Hooks**：`use`\\n+- **Library Hooks**：`useContext`、`useDebugValue`、`useMemo`、`useRef`、`useCallback`、`useId`、`useImperativeHandle`、`useSyncExternalStore`\\n+\\n+### 8.2 useEffect（副作用）\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+import { useEffect, useState } from \'react\';\\n+\\n+function FriendStatus({ userId }) {\\n+  const [isOnline, setIsOnline] = useState(null);\\n+\\n+  useEffect(() => {\\n+    function startSubscription() {\\n+      const id = ChatServer.subscribeToFriendStatus(userId, isOnline =>\\n+        setIsOnline(isOnline)\\n+      );\\n+      return () => ChatServer.unsubscribeFromFriendStatus(userId, id);\\n+    }\\n+    return startSubscription();\\n+  }, [userId]);\\n+\\n+  return (\\n+    <span>{isOnline === null ? \'Loading...\' : isOnline ? \'Online\' : \'Offline\'}</span>\\n+  );\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+要点：\\n+- 副作用放 `useEffect`，**不要**放在渲染体里（渲染阶段应无副作用）。\\n+- 返回函数即\\"清理/取消\\"逻辑，在重新执行前与卸载时调用。\\n+- 依赖数组 `[userId]` 决定何时重新执行；省略则每次渲染都跑，`[]` 则只挂载一次。\\n+\\n+### 8.3 依赖数组与闭包陷阱\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+import { useEffect, useState } from \'react\';\\n+\\n+function Profile({ userId }) {\\n+  const [age, setAge] = useState(0);\\n+\\n+  // 只在 userId 变化时重新拉取\\n+  useEffect(() => {\\n+    setAge(fetchAge(userId));\\n+  }, [userId]);\\n+\\n+  return <p>{userId} is {age}</p>;\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+### 8.4 useReducer（复杂状态）\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+import { useReducer } from \'react\';\\n+\\n+const actions = { TICK: \'TICK\' };\\n+\\n+function reducer(state, action) {\\n+  switch (action) {\\n+    case actions.TICK:\\n+      return state + 1;\\n+    default:\\n+      throw Error(\'Unknown action: \' + action);\\n+  }\\n+}\\n+\\n+export function Ticker() {\\n+  const [count, dispatch] = useReducer(reducer, 0);\\n+  return (\\n+    <>\\n+      <p>Count: {count}</p>\\n+      <button onClick={() => dispatch(actions.TICK)}>+1</button>\\n+    </>\\n+  );\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+### 8.5 useContext（跨层级共享数据）\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+import { createContext, useContext, useState } from \'react\';\\n+\\n+const ThemeContext = createContext(\'light\');\\n+\\n+export function Provider({ children }) {\\n+  const [theme, setTheme] = useState(\'light\');\\n+  return (\\n+    <ThemeContext.Provider value={theme}>\\n       {children}\\n-    </section>\\n-  );\\n-}\\n-```\\n-\\n----\\n-\\n-## 第 4 章 State：让组件有记忆\\n-\\n-```jsx\\n-// 示例 4-1：useState 计数器\\n-import { useState } from </ul>\\n-);\\n-```\\n-\\n----\\n-\\n-## 第 7 章 Hooks 全景\\n-\\n-官方内置 Hooks（React 19）：\\n-\\n-| Hook | 用途 |\\n-|------|------|\\n-| `useState` | 局部状态 |\\n-| `useEffect` | 副作用 |\\n-| `useContext` | 读取上下文 |\\n-| `useReducer` | 复杂状态归约 |\\n-| `useMemo` / `useCallback` | 记忆化 |\\n-| `useRef` | 跨渲染持久引用 / DOM |\\n-| `useLayoutEffect` | 同步布局副作用 |\\n-| `useId` | 生成稳定唯一 id |\\n-| `useDebugValue` | DevTools 调试 |\\n-| `useTransition` / `useDeferredValue` | 并发渲染 |\\n-| `useSyncExternalStore` | 订阅外部存储 |\\n-| `useInsertionEffect` | CSS-in-JS 场景 |\\n-| `useActionState` / `useOptimistic` | 表单与乐观更新 |\\n-\\n-```jsx\\n-// 示例 7-1：useEffect 请求数据\\n-import { useState, useEffect } from \'react\';\\n-\\n-function Posts() {\\n-  const [posts, setPosts] = useState(null);\\n-  const [error, setError] = );\\n+}\\n+\\n+export function Title() {\\n+  const theme = useContext(ThemeContext);\\n+  return <h1 style={{ color: theme }}>Themed Title</h1>;\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+## 9. 自定义 Hook 与复用\\n+\\n+把 Hook 7-2：useEffect 订阅外部事件\\n-import { useEffect } from \'react\';\\n-\\n-function Cursor() {\\n-  const [pos, setPos] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });\\n-  useEffect(() => {\\n-    function onMove(e) {\\n- {pos.y}</span>;\\n-}\\n-```\\n-\\n-```jsx\\n-// 示例 7-3：useMemo / useCallback 记忆化\\n-import { useMemo, useCallback, useState } from \'react\';\\n-\\n-function Filter({ items, query }) {\\n-  const [userId]);\\n+  return isOnline;\\n+}\\n+\\n+function ChatMessage({ userId }) {\\n+  const isOnline = useFriendStatus(userId);\\n+  return <p>{isOnline ? \'Online\' : \'Offline\'}</p>;\\n+}\\n+```\\n+\\n+## 10. 数据流：状态提升与组合\\n+\\n+```jsx\\n+export default function App() {\\n+  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(\'cabbage\');\\n   return (\\n     <div>\\n-      <button onClick={() => dispatch({ type: \'increment\' })}>+1</button>\\n-      <span>{state.count}</span>\\n-      <button onClick={() => dispatch({ type: \'reset\' })}>重置</button>\\n+      <Product name=\\"Cabbage\\" selected={selected} onSelect={setSelected} />\\n+      <Product name=\\"Apple\\" selected={selected} onSelect={setSelected} />\\n+      <Cart selected={selected} />\\n     </div>\\n   );\\n }\\n ```\\n \\n-```jsx\\n-// 示例 7-5：自定义 Hook（复用逻辑）\\n-import { useState, useEffect } from \'react\';\\n-\\n-function useLocalStorage(key, initial) {\\n-  const [value, setValue] = useState(() => {\\n-    const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);\\n-    return raw ? Hook 名称必须以 `use` 开头\\n-\\n-```jsx\\n-// 示例 7-6：错误示范（不合法）\\n-function Bad({ flag }) {\\n-  if (flag) {\\n-    const [x, setX] = useState(0);   // ❌ 条件调用\\n-  }\\n-  return default`。\\n+- 状态尽量下放到**最靠近使用处**的祖先组件，避免全局状态膨胀。\\n+- 列表必带稳定 `key`；不要在渲染体里修改 state。\\n+- 事件处理函数只传递不立即调用。\\n+- 副作用集中在 `useEffect`，并正确书写依赖数组与清理函数。\\n+- 优先函数组件 + Hooks；类组件仅用于兼容旧代码。\\n+- 遵循官方 Rules of 是用于构建用户界面的 JavaScript 库，核心思想是**声明式 UI**：描述界面"应该是什么样"，而非"如何一步步变成那样"。\n-\n-**核心支柱**：\n-\n-| 概念 | 说明 |\n-|------|------|\n-| Components（组件） | 最小 UI 单元，可组合复用 |\n-| Elements（元素） | UI 的最小描述对象 |\n-| 1. 心智模型\n+\n+- React 应用由**组件（Component）**组成：组件是拥有自身逻辑与外观的 UI 片段，可小到按钮、大到整页。\n+- 组件就是**返回 markup 的 JavaScript 函数**。\n+- React 是**声明式**的：你描述"要渲染什么"，React 决定"如何显示"。\n+- 组件与 Hooks 只能由 React 调用（在 JSX 中），**不要像普通函数那样直接调用组件**。\n+\n+## 2. 组件与 JSX\n+\n+```jsx\n+function MyButton() {\n+  return (\n+    <button>I\'m a button</button>\n+  );\n+}\n+```\n+\n+```jsx\n+export default function MyApp() {\n+  return (\n+    <div>\n+      <h1>Welcome to my app</h1>\n+      <MyButton />\n+    </div>\n+  );\n+}\n+```\n+\n+> 约定：组件名必须**大写开头**（`<MyButton />`），HTML 标签小写。`export default` 指定文件主组件。\n+\n+### 2.1 JSX 语法约束\n+\n+JSX 比 HTML 更严格：自闭合标签要写 `<br />`；返回的 JSX 必须有单一父节点（`<div>` 或空片段 `<>...</>`）。\n+\n+```jsx\n+function AboutPage() {\n+  return (\n+    <>\n+      <h1>About</h1>\n+      <p>Hello there.<br />How do you do?</p>\n+    </>\n+  );\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 2.2 样式\n+\n+React 中 CSS 类名用 `className`，规则写在单独的 CSS 文件里；依赖 JS 变量时用 `style`。\n+\n+```css\n+/* In your CSS */\n+.avatar {\n+  border-radius: 50%;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+```jsx\n+const user = {\n+  name: \'Hedy Lamarr\',\n+  imageUrl: \'https://react.dev/images/docs/scientists/yXOvdOSs.jpg\',\n+  imageSize: 90,\n+};\n+\n+export default function Profile() {\n+  return (\n+    <>\n+      <h1>{user.name}</h1>\n+      <img\n+        className="avatar"\n+        src={user.imageUrl}\n+        alt={\'Photo of \' + user.name}\n+        style={{ width: user.imageSize, height: user.imageSize }}\n+      />\n+    </>\n+  );\n+}\n+```\n+\n+> JSX 中用 `{}` 插值 JS 表达式；属性里用 `{}` 而**不是**引号：`className="avatar"` 传字符串，`src={user.imageUrl}` 读变量。\n+\n+## 3. Props 与数据传递\n+\n+```jsx\n+function Greeting({ name, role }) {\n+  return <p>{role} {name}</p>;\n }\n \n export default function App() {\n-  return <Welcome name="World" />;\n-}\n-```\n-\n-### 3.2
